# MCAT Master Curriculum & 1,000-Topic Coverage Tracker

A definitive, granular audit of all created content in **DrillRoom**, mapped against the official AAMC Foundational Concepts and past exam topic blueprints, expanded to an exhaustive **1,000-topic master curriculum**.

> **Purpose:** Use this document to track content coverage across CARS, C/P, B/B, and P/S. Before generating new 90-minute exams, 60-minute exams, or targeted drill modules, consult this tracker to select unchecked `[ ]` topics. Once implemented, mark them as checked `[x]` and cite the file and passage/discrete set ID to ensure zero duplication, rigorous topic breadth, and comprehensive coverage across the entire MCAT syllabus.

## Section I: Critical Analysis & Reasoning Skills (CARS)
### 200 Topics Across Humanities & Social Sciences

### Philosophy, Ethics & Epistemology (Topics 1 - 40)

- [x] **1. Philosophy of Culture & Scientism (Veblen)** — `cars_veblen / exam_cars90_p1`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Critique of natural science hegemony; tension between utilitarian industry and ritualistic academic institutions.
- [x] **2. Religious Orthodoxy & Individual Conscience (Hawthorne)** — `aamc_puritan_society / exam_cars90_p2 / exam_cars60_p6`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Puritan legalism vs subjective moral redemption; the interior psychological toll of institutional ostracism.
- [x] **3. Victorian Literary Aesthetics & Fatalism (Hardy)** — `aamc_understanding_thomas_h / exam_cars90_p3`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Cosmic indifference in naturalistic fiction; human will overwhelmed by evolutionary and economic predetermination.
- [x] **4. Evolutionary Behavioral Ecology & Attachment** — `aamc_maternal_psychology / exam_cars90_p4`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Parental investment theory vs proximate psychological bonding; evolutionary trade-offs in kin altruism.
- [x] **5. Historiography & Eurasian Nomadic Migrations (The Huns)** — `aamc_huns_and_eurasian_hist / exam_cars90_p5`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Methodological bias in Roman imperial accounts of nomadic pastoralists; non-literate military technologies.

  *High-Yield Focus:* State-directed top-down industrialization, dismantling samurai feudalism while inventing nationalistic Shinto imperial cults.

### Political Science, Law, Jurisprudence & Government (Topics 121 - 160)

- [ ] **121. Natural Law vs Legal Positivism (Hart vs Fuller Debate)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* The internal morality of law vs law as sovereign command backed by threat; the problem of Nazi grudge-informer trials.
- [ ] **122. Constitutional Jurisprudence: Originalism vs The Living Constitution**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Antonin Scalia's original public meaning vs evolutionary judicial pragmatism adapting to changing moral consensus.
- [ ] **123. Critical Legal Studies & The Indeterminacy of Law**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Roberto Unger; legal doctrine as inherently contradictory, masking conservative ideological bias behind neutral technical logic.
- [ ] **124. The Adversarial vs Inquisitorial Legal System**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Truth emerging from zealous partisan advocacy and judge-as-referee vs judicial magistrate-led evidentiary investigation.
- [ ] **125. Democratic Theory: Deliberative Democracy vs Aggregative Majoritarianism**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Jürgen Habermas; public reason and communicative consensus vs simple numerical vote tallies vulnerable to polarization.
- [ ] **126. Michel Foucault on Panopticism & The Disciplinary Carceral Society**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Discipline and Punish; internalizing the gaze of power from public physical executions to institutional surveillance.
- [ ] **127. Social Contract Theory: Hobbes' Leviathan vs Locke's Second Treatise**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* State of nature as war of all against all requiring absolute sovereign vs natural rights to life, liberty, and property.
- [ ] **128. Civil Disobedience: Henry David Thoreau & Martin Luther King Jr.**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Letter from Birmingham Jail; an unjust law is no law at all; willing acceptance of legal penalties to prick civic conscience.
- [ ] **129. International Relations: Political Realism vs Liberal Institutionalism**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Mearsheimer's offensive neorealism (anarchy and power balancing) vs Keohane's international treaties and mutual trade interdependence.
- [ ] **130. Neoliberalism vs Keynesian Economic Governance**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Milton Friedman's deregulation, privatization, and monetarism vs state counter-cyclical deficit spending in recessions.
- [ ] **131. Electoral Systems: First-Past-The-Post vs Proportional Representation**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Duverger's Law entrenching two-party duopolies vs multi-party coalition compromises and minoritarian fringe representation.
- [ ] **132. Judicial Review & The Counter-Majoritarian Difficulty (Bickel)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Unelected lifetime judges overturning statutes passed by democratically elected legislatures; checks and balances vs judicial tyranny.
- [ ] **133. First Amendment Jurisprudence: Free Speech & The Incitement Standard**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Brandenburg v. Ohio; speech protected unless directed to inciting imminent lawless action and likely to produce such action.
- [ ] **134. Intellectual Property Rights vs Global Access to Essential Medicines**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* TRIPS flexibilities, compulsory patent licensing, and pharmaceutical corporate profit protection during pandemics.
- [ ] **135. Corporate Personhood & Campaign Finance (Citizens United)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* First Amendment commercial speech rights; corporate political spending as protected political expression vs oligarchy.
- [ ] **136. Executive War Powers & The Imperial Presidency (Schlesinger)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* The War Powers Resolution of 1973; executive circumvention of congressional constitutional authority through covert operations.
- [ ] **137. Welfare State Typologies (Gøsta Esping-Andersen)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Liberal residualist (US/UK) vs Corporatist conservative (Germany) vs Social Democratic universalist (Nordic) welfare regimes.
- [ ] **138. Indigenous Land Rights & Sovereignty Under International Law**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* The Doctrine of Discovery; UN Declaration on the Rights of Indigenous Peoples (UNDRIP) and free, prior, and informed consent.
- [ ] **139. Environmental Regulatory Law & The Precautionary Principle**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Acting to prevent environmental harm under scientific uncertainty vs quantitative cost-benefit risk assessment.
- [ ] **140. Technocracy vs Populism: The Crisis of Democratic Legitimacy**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Governance by credentialed expert administrative agencies vs populist anti-institutional appeals to the uncorrupted 'will of the people'.
- [ ] **141. The Rule of Law vs Rule By Law in Authoritarian Regimes**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Constitutional constraints binding sovereign rulers vs manipulating formal statutes as instruments of state oppression.
- [ ] **142. Universal Human Rights vs Cultural Relativism in Global Governance**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* The 1948 Universal Declaration; Western liberal individualistic bias vs communal, collectivist, and non-Western traditions.
- [ ] **143. Jürgen Habermas & The Structural Transformation of the Public Sphere**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* 18th-century bourgeois coffee houses as rational-critical debate arenas corrupted by commercial mass media public relations.
- [ ] **144. Michelle Alexander & The New Jim Crow: Mass Incarceration**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* The racialized war on drugs; felon disenfranchisement and collateral consequences creating a permanent racial undercaste.
- [ ] **145. Civic Republicanism: Freedom as Non-Domination (Pettit)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Freedom not merely as non-interference (negative liberty), but as security against arbitrary unchecked power (structural independence).
- [ ] **146. Regulatory Capture & The Revolving Door in Administrative Agencies**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Stigler; regulated industries co-opting federal regulatory boards (FDA, EPA, SEC) to construct anti-competitive barriers to entry.
- [ ] **147. Elinor Ostrom & Governing the Commons Beyond State vs Market**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Polycentric governance of shared common-pool resources (fisheries, aquifers) through communal monitoring and graduated sanctions.
- [ ] **148. Giorgio Agamben & The State of Exception: Sovereign Power**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Homo Sacer; emergency declarations suspending the constitutional legal order, reducing humans to disposable 'bare life'.
- [ ] **149. Platform Monopolies & The New Brandeis Antitrust Movement**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Lina Khan; Amazon's predatory pricing and platform dominance challenging traditional Chicago School consumer welfare pricing metrics.
- [ ] **150. Biopolitics & Governmentality: Foucault on Modern State Power**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* The sovereign right to 'make die and let live' shifting to biopolitical state management to 'make live and let die' (population health).
- [ ] **151. Achille Mbembe & Necropolitics: Sovereign Control Over Death**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Colonial frontiers and military occupation zones where sovereign power is operationalized as the right to subjugate populations to living death.
- [ ] **152. Legal Paternalism vs Individual Autonomy in Public Health**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Motorcycle helmet laws, vaccine mandates, and soda taxes; Mill's Harm Principle vs state duty to protect citizen welfare.
- [ ] **153. The Ethics of Whistleblowing & The National Security State**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Edward Snowden; civil service secrecy oaths vs higher democratic obligation to expose illegal government surveillance.
- [ ] **154. Distributive Justice: John Rawls' Difference Principle**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Justice as Fairness; socioeconomic inequalities permitted only if they work to the greatest benefit of the least-advantaged members of society.
- [ ] **155. Libertarian Entitlement Theory: Robert Nozick's Anarchy, State, and Utopia**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Justice in acquisition and transfer; taxation of earnings as forced labor; the minimal state restricted to protection against force and fraud.
- [ ] **156. The Politics of Recognition (Charles Taylor)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Multiculturalism; identity-based political struggles demanding institutional validation of distinct minority cultural heritages.
- [ ] **157. Epistemic Democracy: Condorcet's Jury Theorem**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Collective wisdom of diverse citizen juries outperforming individual experts when individual competence exceeds 0.5.
- [ ] **158. Corporate Social Responsibility (CSR) vs Shareholder Primacy (Friedman)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* The social responsibility of business is to increase profits within the law vs stakeholder capitalism balancing labor and environment.
- [ ] **159. Authoritarian Resilience & The Digital Surveillance State**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* China's Social Credit System; algorithmic censorship, big data facial recognition, and automated state control of dissent.
- [ ] **160. The International Criminal Court (ICC) & The Dilemma of Universal Jurisdiction**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Prosecuting war crimes and genocide; tension between state territorial sovereignty and global judicial accountability.

### Sociology, Anthropology & Cultural Studies (Topics 161 - 200)

- [ ] **161. Clifford Geertz & Thick Description in Cultural Anthropology**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Balinese Cockfight; culture as webs of significance spun by humans; analyzing symbolic meaning over functional mechanics.
- [ ] **162. Claude Lévi-Strauss & Structuralist Anthropology**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Universal binary oppositions (nature vs culture, raw vs cooked) underlying diverse worldwide kinship and mythological systems.
- [ ] **163. Paul Farmer & The Concept of Structural Violence in Healthcare**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Deeply embedded historical social structures (racism, poverty, debt) physically harming individuals and predisposing them to epidemics.
- [ ] **164. Erving Goffman: Stigma & The Management of Spoiled Identity**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Overt physical deformities, moral character flaws, and tribal social stigmas; passing, covering, and social interaction tensions.
- [ ] **165. Robert Putnam: Social Capital & The Decline of Civic Community**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Bowling Alone; bonding social capital (exclusive in-groups) vs bridging social capital (inclusive civic bridges across divides).
- [ ] **166. George Ritzer & The McDonaldization of Modern Society**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Efficiency, calculability, predictability, and control via non-human technology; the irrationality of rationalized hyper-systems.
- [ ] **167. Zygmunt Bauman: Liquid Modernity & Precarious Human Relationships**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Transition from solid, durable institutions to liquid, fluid, temporary consumer networks characterized by systemic precarity.
- [ ] **168. W.E.B. Du Bois: Double Consciousness & The Veil of Race**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* The Souls of Black Folk; the psychological twoness of being an American and a Black person in a hostile racist society.
- [ ] **169. Edward Said & Orientalism: Western Construction of the Near East**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Academic scholarship and colonial literature constructing the 'Orient' as exotic, irrational, feminine, and requiring Western control.
- [ ] **170. Marshall McLuhan: The Medium is the Message**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* The cognitive and sensory restructuring of consciousness caused by communication technologies (print vs television vs internet).
- [ ] **171. Carol Gilligan & The Feminist Ethics of Care**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* In a Different Voice; women's moral development framed around relationships, empathy, and care vs male justice and abstract rules.
- [ ] **172. Victor Turner: Liminality & Communitas in Rites of Passage**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* The transitional threshold state where social hierarchies dissolve, fostering intense egalitarian communal solidarity.
- [ ] **173. Judith Butler: Gender Performativity & Subversion of Identity**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Gender Trouble; gender not as an internal biological essence, but as a repetitive stylized bodily performance enforced by heteronormativity.
- [ ] **174. Ulrich Beck & The Global Risk Society**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Transition from distributing goods (wealth) to distributing 'bads' (manufactured nuclear, chemical, and climate risks beyond national borders).
- [ ] **175. Antonio Gramsci & Cultural Hegemony in Capitalist Democracies**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Ruling class maintaining ideological dominance through civil society institutions (churches, schools, media) engineering popular consent.
- [ ] **176. Gentrification & The Spatial Politics of Urban Displacement**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Capital reinvestment in disinvested inner-city neighborhoods; cultural displacement of working-class and minority communities.
- [ ] **177. The Gig Economy & The Digital Precarity of Platform Labor**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Algorithmic management, piece-rate wages, and evasion of corporate employer obligations by misclassifying workers as contractors.
- [ ] **178. Environmental Racism & Spatial Inequality in Toxic Waste Siting**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Robert Bullard; disproportionate placement of landfills, chemical plants, and refineries in low-income minority communities.
- [ ] **179. Critical Race Theory: Derrick Bell & Racial Realism**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Racism not as an individual aberration, but as a normalized structural feature of legal, economic, and institutional systems.
- [ ] **180. Aníbal Quijano & The Coloniality of Power**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* The Eurocentric colonial classification of human populations into racial hierarchies surviving formal political decolonization.
- [ ] **181. The Social Construction of Illness: Arthur Kleinman's Illness vs Disease**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Biological biomedical disease pathology vs the lived, subjective, culturally shaped human experience of illness.
- [ ] **182. Medicalization of Everyday Human Life: Peter Conrad**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Converting normal behavioral variations (shyness into social anxiety, aging into testosterone deficiency) into clinical diagnoses.
- [ ] **183. Sociology of the Food System: Corporate Consolidation & Food Deserts**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Industrial agriculture, ultra-processed food subsidies, and the urban absence of affordable fresh produce driving chronic disease.
- [ ] **184. The Attention Economy & The Social Engineering of Dopamine**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Digital platforms monetizing human gaze; algorithmic outrage, notification design, and the erosion of sustained focus.
- [ ] **185. Secularization Theory vs The Resurgence of Public Religious Movements**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Classical sociologists' failed prediction that modernization inevitably erodes religion; rise of evangelical and post-secular orthodoxies.
- [ ] **186. Language Revitalization & Linguistic Justice for Indigenous Tongues**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Language loss as cultural epistemicide; indigenous immersion schools recovering ecological and philosophical knowledge.
- [ ] **187. The Sociology of Childhood: Adults' Construction of Innocence (Postman)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Childhood not as a universal biological state, but as a modern social invention eroded by unmediated digital media access.
- [ ] **188. Standpoint Epistemology & Donna Haraway's Situated Knowledges**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Rejecting the 'god trick' of disembodied objectivity; all knowledge is socially situated, with marginalized standpoints offering unique insights.
- [ ] **189. Sociological Perspectives on Death & Dying: Philippe Ariès**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* The transition from medieval 'tamed death' embedded in community to modern sanitized, medicalized, and institutionalized death.
- [ ] **190. Transnationalism & Remittance Economies in Global Migration**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Migrant dual-lives maintaining deep financial, political, and emotional ties across national borders via instant digital transfers.
- [ ] **191. The Informal Economy & Survival Strategies in the Global South**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Street vending, unrecorded trades, and informal settlements (favelas) as dynamic responses to exclusion from formal capitalism.
- [ ] **192. The Social Life of Things: Arjun Appadurai on Commodities**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Objects traveling through commoditized and de-commoditized cultural phases, acquiring distinct social biographies and value.
- [ ] **193. Age Stratification & Ageism in Technocentric Societies**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Economic devaluation of elderly wisdom in rapidly shifting technological economies; structural isolation in retirement facilities.
- [ ] **194. Sociology of Disaster: Rebecca Solnit & A Paradise Built in Hell**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Elite disaster panic vs grass-roots mutual aid and spontaneous communal solidarity emerging in the wake of catastrophes.
- [ ] **195. Feminist Standpoint Theory: Sandra Harding on Strong Objectivity**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Starting scientific inquiry from the lives and experiences of women and marginalized groups produces more rigorous, less biased science.
- [ ] **196. Subcultural Resistance & The Co-optation of Youth Movements (Hebdige)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Punk subculture; style as symbolic defiance against class despair, swiftly co-opted and commodified by commercial fashion markets.
- [ ] **197. Sociology of Body Modification: Tattoos as Identity Narratives**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Tattoos shifting from criminal/deviant markers to mainstream practices of bodily autonomy, memorialization, and self-expression.
- [ ] **198. The Social Construction of Time: Eviatar Zerubavel's Hidden Rhythms**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Seven-day weeks, standard time zones, and liturgical calendars as artificial cultural schedules organizing collective human life.
- [ ] **199. Posthumanism & The Blurring Boundary Between Human and Machine (Haraway)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* A Cyborg Manifesto; dismantling dualisms (human/animal, organism/machine, male/female) in technological consumer society.
- [ ] **200. Global Health Governance & The Geopolitics of Vaccine Apartheid**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Nationalistic vaccine hoarding, intellectual property waivers, and bilateral diplomatic leverage in global public health crises.

### Thermodynamics, Kinetics & Equilibrium (Topics 366 - 400)

- [x] **366. Diffraction Limit of Light & Abbe Resolution Equation**   — `exam_cp90_8_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Minimum resolvable distance d = lambda / (2*NA); numerical aperture NA = n*sin(alpha).

  *High-Yield Focus:* Reagent blank subtracting absorbance contributions of solvent, buffers, and chromogenic precursors.
- [x] **368. Turbidimetry vs Nephelometry in Serum Protein Quantification**   — `exam_cp90_8_disc`

- [x] **369. Refractive Index Matching in Tissue Clearing for 3D Microscopy**   — `exam_cp90_8_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Replacing water/lipids with high-refractive-index solvents (CLARITY, BABB) to render whole mouse brains transparent.
- [x] **370. Flow Cytometry Optical Layout: Forward Scatter (FSC) vs Side Scatter (SSC)**   — `exam_cp90_8_p1 / exam_cp60_8_p1`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* FSC (diffraction at low angles) indicating cell size; SSC (90-degree refraction/reflection) indicating internal granularity.
- [x] **371. Bohr Model of the Hydrogen Atom & Quantized Energy Transitions**   — `exam_cp90_8_p2 / exam_cp60_8_p2`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Delta E = -13.6 eV * (1/nf^2 - 1/ni^2); Lyman (UV), Balmer (visible), and Paschen (IR) emission spectral series.
- [x] **372. Quantum Numbers and Pauli Exclusion Principle**   — `exam_cp90_8_p2 / exam_cp60_8_p2`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Principal (n), angular momentum (l), magnetic (ml), and spin (ms) quantum numbers; no two electrons having identical states.
- [x] **373. Aufbau Principle, Hund's Rule & Transition Metal Anomalies**   — `exam_cp90_8_p2 / exam_cp60_8_p2`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Chromium ([Ar] 4s1 3d5) and Copper ([Ar] 4s1 3d10) half-filled and fully-filled d-subshell thermodynamic stability.
- [x] **374. Effective Nuclear Charge (Zeff) Across Periods and Groups**   — `exam_cp90_8_p3 / exam_cp60_8_p3`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Zeff = Z - S; core electron shielding explaining periodic contractions in atomic radius from left to right.
- [x] **375. Periodic Trends: Atomic vs Ionic Radii (Cations vs Anions)**   — `exam_cp90_8_p3 / exam_cp60_8_p3`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Cations smaller than neutral parent (electron loss, increased Zeff); anions larger than parent (inter-electron repulsion).
- [x] **376. Periodic Trends: First vs Second Ionization Energy**   — `exam_cp90_8_p3 / exam_cp60_8_p3`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Massive second IE spike for alkali metals (Group 1) upon stripping stable noble gas core electron configurations.
- [x] **377. Periodic Trends: Electron Affinity and Electronegativity Scales**   — `exam_cp90_8_p3 / exam_cp60_8_p3`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Halogen high exothermic electron affinity; Pauling electronegativity scale (Fluorine = 4.0 to Francium = 0.7).
- [x] **378. Paramagnetism vs Diamagnetism in Transition Metal Complexes**   — `exam_cp90_8_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Unpaired electrons producing attraction to external magnetic fields; diamagnetic species (all paired) weakly repelled.
- [x] **379. Nuclear Binding Energy, Mass Defect & Einstein's E = mc^2**   — `exam_cp90_8_p4 / exam_cp60_8_p4`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Nuclear mass strictly less than sum of constituent nucleons; mass defect converted to binding energy during fusion.
- [x] **380. Alpha Decay Kinetics & Nuclear Transmutation Balancing**   — `exam_cp90_8_p4 / exam_cp60_8_p4`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Loss of 4-He nucleus (A decreases by 4, Z decreases by 2); heavy nucleus daughter isotope identification.
- [x] **381. Beta-Minus Decay & Antineutrino Conservation**   — `exam_cp90_8_p4 / exam_cp60_8_p4`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Neutron converts to proton (n -> p + e- + anti-v); atomic number Z increases by 1, mass number A remains constant.
- [x] **382. Beta-Plus (Positron) Decay & Positron-Electron Annihilation**   — `exam_cp90_8_p4 / exam_cp60_8_p4`

- [x] **383. Electron Capture Decay Mode in Heavy Elements**   — `exam_cp90_8_p4 / exam_cp60_8_p4`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Inner core shell electron captured by nucleus (p + e- -> n + v); X-ray emission as outer electrons drop into vacancy.
- [x] **384. First Law of Thermodynamics: Delta U = Q - W**   — `exam_cp90_8_p5 / exam_cp60_8_p5`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Work done BY system (W = P*Delta V) subtracting from internal energy; adiabatic expansion (Q = 0) cooling gases.
- [x] **385. PV Diagrams & Thermodynamic Cycles (Carnot, Otto, Brayton)**   — `exam_cp90_8_p5 / exam_cp60_8_p5`

- [x] **386. Hess's Law & Standard Enthalpies of Formation (Delta Hf°)**   — `exam_cp90_8_p6`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Delta H_rxn° = Sigma n*Delta Hf°(products) - Sigma m*Delta Hf°(reactants); state function path independence.
- [x] **387. Bond Dissociation Energies & Reaction Enthalpy Estimation**   — `exam_cp90_8_p6`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Delta H_rxn = Sigma BDE(bonds broken) - Sigma BDE(bonds formed); all BDE values positive endothermic inputs.
- [x] **388. Heating and Cooling Curves & Latent Heat Constants**   — `exam_cp90_8_p7`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Q = m*c*Delta T during single phase warming; Q = m*L_fusion or m*L_vaporization at phase change plateaus.
- [x] **389. Phase Diagrams: Critical Point & Supercritical Fluid Chromatography**   — `exam_cp90_8_p7`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Supercritical CO2 combining liquid-like density with gas-like low viscosity for green pharmaceutical extractions.
- [x] **390. Water's Anomalous Negative-Sloping Solid-Liquid Phase Boundary**   — `exam_cp90_9_p1 / exam_cp60_9_p1`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Open hexagonal ice crystal lattice being less dense than liquid water; pressure melting ice (skating mechanics).
- [x] **391. Clausius-Clapeyron Equation & Temperature-Vapor Pressure Curves**   — `exam_cp90_9_p1 / exam_cp60_9_p1`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* ln(P2/P1) = -(Delta H_vap / R) * (1/T2 - 1/T1); boiling occurring when vapor pressure equals ambient atmospheric pressure.
- [x] **392. Second Law of Thermodynamics: Entropy of Isolated Systems**   — `exam_cp90_9_p2 / exam_cp60_9_p2`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Delta S_universe = Delta S_system + Delta S_surroundings > 0 for all spontaneous natural processes; microstates W (S = k*ln W).
- [x] **393. Third Law of Thermodynamics & Absolute Zero Entropy Reference**   — `exam_cp90_9_p2 / exam_cp60_9_p2`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Entropy of a pure, perfectly crystalline substance at absolute zero (0 K) is identically zero; standard molar entropies S°.
- [x] **394. Gibbs Free Energy Equation & Temperature-Dependent Spontaneity**   — `exam_cp90_9_p2 / exam_cp60_9_p2`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Delta G = Delta H - T*Delta S; spontaneous at all temps (-Delta H, +Delta S) vs non-spontaneous at all temps (+Delta H, -Delta S).
- [x] **395. Thermodynamic vs Kinetic Reaction Control in Organic Synthesis**   — `exam_cp90_9_p3 / exam_cp60_9_p3`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Lower activation energy kinetic product favored at low temps/short times; lower free energy thermodynamic product at high temps.
- [x] **396. Relationship Between Standard Free Energy & Equilibrium Constant**   — `exam_cp90_9_p3 / exam_cp60_9_p3`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Delta G° = -RT*ln(K); K > 1 corresponding to Delta G° < 0; K < 1 corresponding to Delta G° > 0.
- [x] **397. Non-Standard Free Energy & Reaction Quotient: Delta G = Delta G° + RT*ln(Q)**   — `exam_cp90_9_p3 / exam_cp60_9_p3`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Determining spontaneous direction under cellular physiological metabolite concentrations; Q < K shifting forward.
- [x] **398. Coupled Biochemical Reactions: ATP Phosphoanhydride Hydrolysis**   — `exam_cp90_9_p4 / exam_cp60_9_p4`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* ATP + H2O -> ADP + Pi (Delta G°' = -30.5 kJ/mol); coupling to endergonic reactions via common phosphorylated intermediates.
- [x] **399. Law of Mass Action & Equilibrium Constant Expressions (Kc vs Kp)**   — `exam_cp90_9_p4 / exam_cp60_9_p4`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Excluding pure solids and pure liquids from equilibrium expressions; Kp = Kc * (RT)^(Delta n_gas).
- [x] **400. Le Châtelier's Principle: Pressure & Volume Perturbations on Gases**   — `exam_cp90_9_p4 / exam_cp60_9_p4`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Compression shifting equilibrium toward side with fewer total moles of gas; inert gas at constant volume having zero effect.

  *High-Yield Focus:* 2 H2O <=> H3O+ + OH-; Kw = 1.0 x 10^-14 at 25 °C (pH 7.0 = neutral); Kw = 2.4 x 10^-14 at 37 °C (neutral pH = 6.8).

- [ ] **449. Extraction Protocols: Separating Carboxylic Acids, Phenols, and Amines**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Aqueous NaHCO3 selectively deprotonating strong carboxylic acids (R-COOH -> R-COO-); aqueous NaOH deprotonating phenols; HCl protonating amines.
- [ ] **450. Chromatographic Separation Principles: Thin-Layer (TLC) & Retention Factor (Rf)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Silica gel polar stationary phase vs nonpolar organic mobile phase; polar analytes having lower Rf values; visualization via UV/iodine.
- [ ] **451. Gas Chromatography (GC) Retention Times & Volatility**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Separation based on boiling point volatility and stationary liquid phase polarity; lower boiling point compounds eluting first.
- [ ] **452. High-Performance Liquid Chromatography (HPLC): Normal vs Reverse-Phase**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Reverse-phase HPLC: nonpolar C18 stationary phase, polar aqueous mobile phase; polar hydrophilic compounds eluting first.
- [ ] **453. Fractional vs Simple Distillation Mechanics**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Simple distillation for boiling point differences > 25 °C; fractional column packed with beads providing multiple theoretical plates.
- [ ] **454. Vacuum Distillation for High-Boiling Thermolabile Compounds**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Lowering ambient atmospheric pressure inside apparatus to reduce boiling points below decomposition temperatures.
- [ ] **455. Recrystallization Solvent Selection & Crystal Lattice Purification**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Solute completely soluble in hot solvent, insoluble in ice-cold solvent; impurities remaining dissolved in mother liquor.
- [ ] **456. Fischer Esterification Mechanism under Acid Catalysis**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Carboxylic acid + alcohol + catalytic H2SO4; protonation of carbonyl, alcohol nucleophilic attack, tetrahedral intermediate, water loss.
- [ ] **457. Lactones and Lactams: Cyclic Esters and Amides**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Intramolecular condensation of hydroxy-acids (gamma- and delta-lactones); beta-lactam ring strain in penicillin antibiotics.
- [ ] **458. Decarboxylation of Beta-Keto Acids and Malonic Acid Derivatives**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Six-membered cyclic pericyclic transition state releasing CO2 gas and enol intermediate, tautomerizing to stable ketone.
- [ ] **459. Grignard Reagents and Organolithium Carbon-Carbon Bond Formations**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* R-MgBr acting as powerful carbanion nucleophile; requiring anhydrous ether solvents; reacting with carbonyls to yield alcohols.
- [ ] **460. Reduction of Esters and Carboxylic Acids by LiAlH4 vs NaBH4**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Lithium aluminum hydride (LiAlH4) reducing esters/acids to primary alcohols; sodium borohydride (NaBH4) reducing only aldehydes/ketones.
- [ ] **461. Diazonium Salt Chemistry and Sandmeyer Reactions**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Aromatic primary amine + HNO2 -> aryldiazonium salt (Ar-N2+); nucleophilic displacement yielding aryl halides and phenols.
- [ ] **462. Wittig Reaction: Carbonyl Olefination via Phosphonium Ylides**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Aldehyde/ketone + triphenylphosphonium ylide -> oxaphosphetane four-membered ring intermediate -> alkene + triphenylphosphine oxide.
- [ ] **463. Epoxide Ring-Opening Mechanics: Acid vs Base Catalysis**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Acid catalysis: nucleophile attacking more substituted carbon (carbocation-like); base catalysis: nucleophile attacking less hindered carbon (SN2).
- [ ] **464. Diels-Alder [4+2] Cycloaddition Pericyclic Reactions**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Conjugated s-cis diene + dienophile (alkene) -> cyclohexene adduct; concerted mechanism preserving stereochemistry; endo rule.
- [ ] **465. Acidity and Basicity of Nitrogen Heterocycles: Pyridine vs Pyrrole**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Pyridine nitrogen lone pair localized in sp2 orbital (basic); pyrrole nitrogen lone pair participating in 6-pi aromatic sextet (non-basic).
- [ ] **466. Carbohydrate Nomenclature: D- vs L-Sugars and Aldoses vs Ketoses**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Fischer projection highest-numbered chiral center (-OH on right = D); glucose, galactose, mannose C4/C2 epimeric relationships.
- [ ] **467. Haworth Projections and Carbohydrate Mutarotation**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Alpha-anomer (axial -OH trans to CH2OH) vs beta-anomer (equatorial -OH cis to CH2OH); mutarotation via open-chain aldehyde.
- [ ] **468. Reducing vs Non-Reducing Disaccharides & Benedict's Test**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Free hemiacetal anomeric carbon capable of mutarotating into open aldehyde (maltose, lactose = reducing); sucrose = non-reducing.
- [ ] **469. Fatty Acid Saponification & Micelle Formation in Aqueous Media**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Sodium salts of long-chain fatty acids (amphipathic carboxylates); critical micelle concentration (CMC) sequestering hydrophobic dirt.
- [ ] **470. Resolving Racemic Mixtures into Pure Enantiomers**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Reacting racemic acid with enantiopure chiral base to yield separable diastereomeric salts with distinct melting points and solubilities.

  *High-Yield Focus:* Fluoroacetate converted to fluorocitrate, acting as a potent mechanism-based competitive inhibitor of aconitase, arresting the TCA cycle.
- [x] **547. Citric Acid Cycle Anaplerotic Reactions: Pyruvate Carboxylase** — `exam_bb90_5_disc / exam_bb60_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Replenishing oxaloacetate (OAA) depleted by biosynthetic pathways; absolute obligate allosteric activation of pyruvate carboxylase by acetyl-CoA.
- [x] **548. Metabolic Compartmentalization: Mitochondrial Matrix vs Cytosol vs Peroxisome** — `exam_bb90_5_disc / exam_bb60_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Glycolysis, PPP, fatty acid synthesis in cytosol; TCA, beta-oxidation, ketogenesis in matrix; VLCFA oxidation in peroxisomes; urea cycle split.
- [x] **549. Regulation of Glycogenolysis: Calcium Activation of Phosphorylase Kinase** — `exam_bb90_5_disc / exam_bb60_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Nerve impulse Ca2+ influx binding calmodulin subunit of phosphorylase kinase, synchronizing muscle contraction with glycogen mobilization.
- [x] **550. Debranching Enzyme Bifunctional Catalysis: 4:4 Transferase & Alpha-1,6-Glucosidase** — `exam_bb90_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Transferring maltotriosyl unit to linear non-reducing end, then hydrolyzing remaining single alpha(1->6) glucosyl residue as free glucose.

### Metabolism, Bioenergetics, Carbohydrates & Lipids (Topics 551 - 605)

- [x] **551. Substrate Flux in the Well-Fed Absorptive State** — `exam_bb90_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* High insulin / glucagon ratio; hepatic glycogenesis, glycolysis, lipogenesis (VLDL export); muscle glucose uptake via GLUT4; protein synthesis.
- [x] **552. Substrate Flux in the Early Post-Absorptive Fasting State (12-24 Hours)** — `exam_bb90_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Glucagon secretion; hepatic glycogenolysis (primary source of blood glucose) and early gluconeogenesis; adipose lipolysis (free fatty acids).
- [x] **553. Substrate Flux in Prolonged Starvation (> 3 Days)** — `exam_bb90_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Hepatic glycogen stores depleted; renal and hepatic gluconeogenesis; brain metabolic adaptation to ketone bodies (acetoacetate/beta-hydroxybutyrate).
- [x] **554. Fatty Acid De Novo Synthesis: Acetyl-CoA Carboxylase (ACC) Regulation** — `exam_bb90_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Biotin-dependent carboxylation of acetyl-CoA to malonyl-CoA; rate-limiting step; activated by citrate and insulin; inhibited by palmitoyl-CoA and glucagon.
- [x] **555. Fatty Acid Synthase (FAS) Multi-Enzyme Complex & NADPH Consumption** — `exam_bb90_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Acyl carrier protein (ACP) phosphopantetheine arm; repeating condensation, reduction, dehydration, reduction cycles consuming 2 NADPH per 2-carbon unit.
- [x] **556. Citrate Malate Pyruvate Shuttle for Cytosolic Acetyl-CoA Transport** — `exam_bb90_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Mitochondrial acetyl-CoA condensing with OAA to citrate, exported through tricarboxylate transport protein, cleaved by ATP-citrate lyase in cytosol.
- [x] **557. Lipoprotein Classification & Apolipoprotein Functions (Chylomicrons, VLDL, LDL, HDL)**   — `exam_bb90_6_p1 / exam_bb60_6_p1`
  *High-Yield Focus:* ApoB-48 in chylomicrons; ApoB-100 in VLDL/LDL; ApoC-II activating lipoprotein lipase (LPL); ApoE for hepatic remnant clearance; ApoA-I in HDL.
- [x] **558. Reverse Cholesterol Transport & High-Density Lipoproteins (HDL)**   — `exam_bb90_6_p2 / exam_bb60_6_p2`
  *High-Yield Focus:* ApoA-I activating lecithin-cholesterol acyltransferase (LCAT / PCAT) to esterify cholesterol; CETP transfer to VLDL; atheroprotective mechanism.
- [x] **559. Low-Density Lipoprotein (LDL) Receptor Endocytosis & Familial Hypercholesterolemia**   — `exam_bb90_6_p3 / exam_bb60_6_p3`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Receptor-mediated endocytosis via clathrin-coated pits; lysosomal degradation of ApoB-100; genetic LDL receptor deficiency causing severe xanthomas.
- [x] **560. Cholesterol Biosynthesis: HMG-CoA Reductase & Statin Pharmacodynamics**   — `exam_bb90_6_p4 / exam_bb60_6_p4`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Cytosolic HMG-CoA reduced to mevalonate consuming 2 NADPH; rate-limiting committed step; statins acting as reversible competitive inhibitors.
- [x] **561. Bile Acid Synthesis, Enterohepatic Circulation & Cholesterol Elimination**   — `exam_bb90_6_p5 / exam_bb60_6_p5`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Cholesterol 7-alpha-hydroxylase (CYP7A1); conjugation with glycine/taurine (cholic acid, chenodeoxycholic acid); 95% reabsorbed in terminal ileum.
- [x] **562. Triacylglycerol Mobilization: Hormone-Sensitive Lipase (HSL) vs Lipoprotein Lipase (LPL)**   — `exam_bb90_6_p6`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Adipose HSL activated by glucagon/epinephrine PKA phosphorylation; capillary endothelial LPL activated by insulin and ApoC-II.
- [x] **563. Odd-Chain Fatty Acid Oxidation & Succinyl-CoA Entry into the TCA Cycle**   — `exam_bb90_6_p7`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Final thiolysis yielding propionyl-CoA (3 carbons); propionyl-CoA carboxylase (biotin) -> methylmalonyl-CoA mutase (B12) -> succinyl-CoA.
- [x] **564. Peroxisomal Alpha-Oxidation of Branched-Chain Phytanic Acid & Refsum Disease**   — `exam_bb90_6_disc / exam_bb60_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Phytanic acid alpha-hydroxylase deficiency preventing degradation of dairy/meat dietary chlorophyll derivatives; cerebellar ataxia and retinitis pigmentosa.
- [x] **565. Microsomal Omega-Oxidation of Fatty Acids to Dicarboxylic Acids**   — `exam_bb90_6_disc / exam_bb60_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Endoplasmic reticulum cytochrome P450 (CYP4A) oxidizes terminal methyl carbon (omega carbon) to dicarboxylic acids when beta-oxidation is impaired.
- [x] **566. Ketogenesis Stoichiometry: Acetoacetate and Beta-Hydroxybutyrate**   — `exam_bb90_6_disc / exam_bb60_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Mitochondrial HMG-CoA synthase and lyase producing acetoacetate; beta-hydroxybutyrate dehydrogenase interconversion based on [NADH]/[NAD+] ratio.
- [x] **567. Ketoacidosis vs Ketosis in Diabetic and Starvation States**   — `exam_bb90_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Diabetic ketoacidosis (DKA): severe insulin deficiency driving uninhibited lipolysis, massive hepatic ketogenesis, systemic metabolic acidosis, anion gap > 20.
- [x] **568. Transamination Reactions: Alanine Aminotransferase (ALT) and Aspartate Aminotransferase (AST)**   — `exam_bb90_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Glutamate + Pyruvate <=> alpha-Ketoglutarate + Alanine (ALT); Glutamate + Oxaloacetate <=> alpha-Ketoglutarate + Aspartate (AST); PLP cofactor.
- [x] **569. Oxidative Deamination: Glutamate Dehydrogenase in the Mitochondrial Matrix**   — `exam_bb90_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Glutamate + NAD+ + H2O <=> alpha-Ketoglutarate + NH4+ + NADH; allosterically inhibited by GTP/ATP; releasing free toxic ammonium ions.
- [x] **570. The Urea Cycle: Carbamoyl Phosphate Synthetase I (CPS-I) Committed Step**   — `exam_bb90_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Mitochondrial matrix CPS-I consuming 2 ATP to condense NH4+ and HCO3-; obligate allosteric activation by N-acetylglutamate (NAG).
- [x] **571. Urea Cycle Compartmentalization: Matrix vs Cytosolic Steps**   — `exam_bb90_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Mitochondrial: CPS-I, Ornithine transcarbamoylase (OTC); Cytosolic: Argininosuccinate synthetase, Argininosuccinase, Arginase-1 releasing urea.
- [x] **572. Hyperammonemia Pathophysiology & Ornithine Transcarbamoylase (OTC) Deficiency**   — `exam_bb90_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* X-linked recessive OTC deficiency; accumulating carbamoyl phosphate shunted into pyrimidine synthesis (elevated urinary orotic acid); cerebral edema.
- [x] **573. Glucogenic vs Ketogenic Amino Acid Catabolism**   — `exam_bb90_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Purely ketogenic: Leucine and Lysine (catabolized exclusively to acetyl-CoA/acetoacetate); Both: Phenylalanine, Tyrosine, Tryptophan, Isoleucine, Threonine.
- [x] **574. Phenylketonuria (PKU): Phenylalanine Hydroxylase Deficiency & BH4**  — `exam_bb90_7_p1 / exam_bb60_7_p1`
  *High-Yield Focus:* PAH deficiency or tetrahydrobiopterin (BH4) cofactor defect; phenylalanine accumulation transaminated to phenylketones; musty odor, severe cognitive deficit.
- [x] **575. Maple Syrup Urine Disease (MSUD): Branched-Chain Alpha-Keto Acid Dehydrogenase**  — `exam_bb90_7_p2 / exam_bb60_7_p2`
  *High-Yield Focus:* BCKDH complex deficiency blocking degradation of branched-chain amino acids (Leucine, Isoleucine, Valine); sweet maple syrup odor, neurotoxicity.

  *High-Yield Focus:* CBS enzyme defect converting homocysteine to cystathionine; elevated plasma homocysteine, marfanoid habitus, ectopia lentis, severe thromboembolism.
- [x] **577. Alkaptonuria: Homogentisate 1,2-Dioxygenase Deficiency & Ochronosis**  — `exam_bb90_7_disc / exam_bb60_7_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Defective tyrosine degradation; accumulating homogentisic acid polymerizing into dark pigment deposited in cartilage and connective tissues; black urine.
- [x] **578. Purine De Novo Synthesis: PRPP Synthetase & Glutamine-PRPP Amidotransferase**  — `exam_bb90_7_p4 / exam_bb60_7_p4`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Ribose-5-phosphate activated to PRPP; purine ring built directly on sugar backbone yielding inosine monophosphate (IMP); feedback inhibited by AMP/GMP.
- [x] **579. Pyrimidine De Novo Synthesis: Cytosolic Carbamoyl Phosphate Synthetase II (CPS-II)**  — `exam_bb90_7_p5 / exam_bb60_7_p5`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Glutamine + CO2 + 2 ATP -> carbamoyl phosphate; pyrimidine ring (orotic acid) synthesized first, then attached to PRPP; inhibited by UTP.
- [x] **580. Purine Salvage Pathway: HGPRT & Lesch-Nyhan Syndrome**  — `exam_bb90_7_p6`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Hypoxanthine-guanine phosphoribosyltransferase recycling free purine bases; X-linked HGPRT deficiency causing PRPP accumulation, uric acid gout, self-mutilation.
- [x] **581. Uric Acid Catabolism & Gout Pharmacotherapy (Allopurinol)**  — `exam_bb90_7_disc / exam_bb60_7_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Xanthine oxidase oxidizing hypoxanthine to xanthine and uric acid; allopurinol suicide inhibitor of xanthine oxidase, preventing urate crystal precipitation in joints.
- [x] **582. Ribonucleotide Reductase (RNR) Mechanism & dNTP Biosynthesis**  — `exam_bb90_7_p7`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Reduction of ribonucleoside diphosphates (NDPs) to deoxyribonucleoside diphosphates (dNDPs); allosterically regulated by ATP (activity) and dATP (inhibition).
- [x] **583. Thymidylate Synthase & Dihydrofolate Reductase (DHFR) in Chemotherapy**  — `exam_bb90_7_p7`
  *High-Yield Focus:* 5-Fluorouracil (5-FU) suicide-inhibiting thymidylate synthase (dUMP -> dTMP); Methotrexate competitively inhibiting DHFR, depleting tetrahydrofolate.

  *High-Yield Focus:* Classic galactosemia (GALT deficiency): accumulation of Gal-1-P and galactitol; infantile cataracts, hepatomegaly, failure to thrive, jaundice.

  *High-Yield Focus:* Aldolase B deficiency trapping toxic fructose-1-phosphate in liver; intracellular Pi depletion halting glycogenolysis and gluconeogenesis; profound hypoglycemia.

  *High-Yield Focus:* Ethanol -> Acetaldehyde (ADH) -> Acetate (ALDH); high [NADH]/[NAD+] ratio inhibiting gluconeogenesis (pyruvate -> lactate), driving hypoglycemia and steatosis.
- [x] **587. Disulfiram (Antabuse) Mechanism: Aldehyde Dehydrogenase Inhibition**  — `exam_bb90_7_disc`

- [x] **589. Carnitine Palmitoyltransferase-2 (CPT-2) Deficiency**  — `exam_bb90_7_disc`

- [x] **590. Primary Carnitine Deficiency: OCTN2 Carnitine Transporter Defect**  — `exam_bb90_7_disc`

- [x] **591. Brown Adipose Tissue (BAT) vs White Adipose Tissue (WAT) Bioenergetics**  — `exam_bb90_7_disc`

  *High-Yield Focus:* Ceramide backbone (sphingosine + fatty acid); sphingomyelin in myelin sheath; cerebrosides (neutral monosaccharide); gangliosides (acidic sialic acid NANA).
- [x] **601. Lysosomal Sphingolipidoses: Tay-Sachs Disease (Hexosaminidase A)**  — `exam_bb90_8_p6`

  *High-Yield Focus:* Most common lysosomal storage disease; glucocerebroside accumulation in macrophages ('crumpled tissue paper' histiocytes); hepatosplenomegaly, bone crises.
