### Molecular Biology, Genetics, DNA Replication & Repair (Topics 606 - 650)

- [x] **606. Eukaryotic Replication Fork: Pol alpha, Pol delta, Pol epsilon & PCNA**  — `exam_bb90_8_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Pol alpha/primase synthesizes RNA/DNA primer; Pol epsilon synthesizes leading strand; Pol delta synthesizes lagging strand Okazaki fragments with PCNA sliding clamp.
- [x] **607. DNA Topoisomerase I and II (Gyrase) Mechanisms & Inhibitors**  — `exam_bb90_8_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Topoisomerase I (single-strand nick, campothecin target); Topoisomerase II (double-strand cut, etoposide/doxorubicin and bacterial ciprofloxacin targets).
- [x] **608. Telomere Maintenance, T-Loop Structure & Shelterin Complex**  — `exam_bb90_8_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* TTAGGG tandem hexanucleotide repeats; 3' single-stranded overhang invading double strand forming protective t-loop capped by shelterin protein complex.
- [x] **609. Telomerase Reverse Transcriptase (TERT) & Human Cellular Senescence**  — `exam_bb90_8_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Ribonucleoprotein enzyme containing internal RNA template (TERC); highly active in germ cells and > 90% of malignant cancers; inactive in somatic tissues.
- [x] **610. DNA Mismatch Repair (MMR) & Lynch Syndrome / HNPCC Genetics**  — `exam_bb90_8_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* MutS/MutL homolog heterodimers (MSH2, MSH6, MLH1, PMS2) recognizing base mismatches and insertion/deletion loops; microsatellite instability (MSI).
- [x] **611. Base Excision Repair (BER): DNA Glycosylases & AP Endonuclease**  — `exam_bb90_8_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Specific DNA glycosylase cleaving N-glycosidic bond of oxidized or deaminated base (uracil-DNA glycosylase); AP endonuclease cleaving 5' phosphodiester bond.
- [x] **612. Double-Strand Break Repair: Homologous Recombination (HR) & BRCA1/BRCA2**  — `exam_bb90_9_p1 / exam_bb60_9_p1`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Error-free S/G2 repair utilizing sister chromatid template; MRN complex resection (3' overhangs), Rad51 recombinase strand invasion; synthetic lethality with PARP inhibitors.
- [x] **613. Double-Strand Break Repair: Non-Homologous End Joining (NHEJ) & Ku70/80**  — `exam_bb90_9_p1 / exam_bb60_9_p1`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Ku70/80 heterodimer binding broken DNA ends, recruiting DNA-PKcs and Artemis endonuclease; error-prone direct ligation by Ligase IV-XRCC4; causes deletions/translocations.
- [x] **614. Chemical DNA Mutagens: Alkylating Agents & Cross-Linking Nitrogen Mustards**  — `exam_bb90_9_p2 / exam_bb60_9_p2`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* O6-methylguanine mispairing with thymine during replication; bifunctional alkylating agents (cyclophosphamide) forming interstrand covalent cross-links.
- [x] **615. Somatic vs Germline Mutations & Evolutionary Heritability**  — `exam_bb90_9_p2 / exam_bb60_9_p2`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Somatic mutations confined to individual tissues (drives carcinogenesis, cannot be inherited); germline mutations present in gametes, transmitted to all offspring cells.
- [x] **616. Mendelian Autosomal Dominant Inheritance & Achondroplasia**  — `exam_bb90_9_p3 / exam_bb60_9_p3`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Heterozygotes affected; 50% recurrence risk per pregnancy; vertical pedigree transmission; FGFR3 gain-of-function mutation; incomplete penetrance and variable expressivity.
- [x] **617. Mendelian Autosomal Recessive Inheritance & Cystic Fibrosis (CFTR)**  — `exam_bb90_9_p3 / exam_bb60_9_p3`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Horizontal pedigree pattern (affected siblings with normal parents); 25% recurrence risk; CFTR delta-F508 misfolding mutation; carrier screening.
- [x] **618. X-Linked Recessive Inheritance: Hemophilia A & Duchenne Muscular Dystrophy**  — `exam_bb90_9_p4 / exam_bb60_9_p4`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Males predominantly affected; no male-to-male transmission; carrier mothers transmitting mutant allele to 50% of sons; skewed lyonization in females.
- [x] **619. X-Linked Dominant Inheritance: Fragile X & Rett Syndrome**  — `exam_bb90_9_p4 / exam_bb60_9_p4`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Affected fathers transmitting trait to 100% of daughters and 0% of sons; affected mothers transmitting to 50% of offspring; trinucleotide CGG repeats.
- [x] **620. Mitochondrial Maternal Inheritance & Heteroplasmy**  — `exam_bb90_9_p4 / exam_bb60_9_p4`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Transmitted exclusively through oocyte cytoplasm; mother transmits to all children; variable phenotypic severity depending on threshold proportion of mutant mtDNA (heteroplasmy).
- [x] **621. Genetic Penetrance vs Expressivity in Human Disease**  — `exam_bb90_9_p5 / exam_bb60_9_p5`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Penetrance (percentage of individuals with genotype who exhibit the clinical phenotype); Expressivity (severity or spectrum of symptoms among individuals with same genotype).
- [x] **622. Genetic Linkage & Recombination Frequency (Centimorgans / Map Units)**  — `exam_bb90_9_p5 / exam_bb60_9_p5`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Genes located close together on same chromosome violating independent assortment; 1% recombination frequency = 1 map unit (cM); max recombination = 50%.
- [x] **623. Eukaryotic Pre-mRNA Splicing: Spliceosome Assembly & Lariat Intermediate**  — `exam_bb90_9_p6`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* U1 snRNP binding 5' splice site (GU), U2 snRNP binding branch point adenosine, U4/U6/U5 tri-snRNP; 2'-5' phosphodiester bond forming intron lariat.
- [x] **624. Alternative Splicing: Exon Skipping & Mutual Exclusion**  — `exam_bb90_9_p6`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Single pre-mRNA primary transcript spliced into multiple distinct mature mRNA isoforms; expanded proteomic diversity (e.g., muscle vs non-muscle tropomyosin).
- [x] **625. RNA Editing: Apolipoprotein B-100 vs B-48 Cytidine Deamination**  — `exam_bb90_9_p6`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* APOBEC-1 cytidine deaminase in intestine deaminating CAA (Gln) to UAA (stop codon), truncating full-length ApoB-100 into ApoB-48 chylomicron protein.
- [x] **626. The Genetic Code: Wobble Hypothesis & Inosine Base-Pairing**  — `exam_bb90_9_p7`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Non-standard Watson-Crick base-pairing at 3rd base of codon (5' base of tRNA anticodon); inosine base-pairing with A, U, or C; explains degeneracy.
- [x] **627. Aminoacyl-tRNA Synthetase Fidelity & The Double-Sieve Proofreading Mechanism**  — `exam_bb90_9_p7`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Activation site selecting amino acids by size/charge; hydrolytic editing pocket hydrolyzing misactivated smaller amino acids (e.g., valine misactivated by isoleucyl-tRNA synthetase).
- [x] **628. Ribosome Structure & Peptidyl Transferase Ribozyme (28S/23S rRNA)**  — `exam_bb90_9_disc / exam_bb60_9_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Aminoglycosides binding 30S A-site causing misreading; Tetracycline blocking aminoacyl-tRNA binding to A-site; Chloramphenicol inhibiting 50S peptidyl transferase.
- [x] **629. Eukaryotic Translation Initiation & eIF2alpha Phosphorylation Stress Response**  — `exam_bb90_9_disc / exam_bb60_9_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Ricin (castor bean) depurinating a single conserved adenine in 28S rRNA; Diphtheria toxin ADP-ribosylating eukaryotic elongation factor-2 (eEF-2), halting translation.
- [x] **630. Translation Elongation Factors (eEF1A / eEF2) & Diphtheria Toxin ADP-Ribosylation**  — `exam_bb90_9_disc / exam_bb60_9_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Cellular stress kinases (PERK, PKR) phosphorylating eIF-2alpha, sequestering eIF-2B guanine nucleotide exchange factor, shutting down global protein synthesis.
- [x] **631. Nonsense-Mediated mRNA Decay (NMD) & Exon Junction Complexes (EJCs)**  — `exam_bb90_9_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* High tryptophan levels allow rapid translation of leader peptide, forming 3-4 transcription terminator hairpin; low tryptophan stalls ribosome at Trp codons, forming 2-3 anti-terminator.
- [x] **632. Post-Translational Glycosylation: ER N-Linked vs Golgi O-Linked**  — `exam_bb90_9_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* HDACs removing acetyl groups, restoring positive charges on lysine residues, condensing chromatin into transcriptionally silent heterochromatin.
- [x] **633. Ubiquitin-Proteasome System: E1, E2, and E3 Ubiquitin Ligase Specificity**  — `exam_bb90_9_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* DNMT3A/3B establishing de novo methylation on cytosines in CpG islands; DNMT1 maintaining methylation patterns across replication; recruiting methyl-CpG binding proteins (MeCP2).
- [x] **634. Zymogen Activation Cascades: Duodenal Enteropeptidase & Pancreatic Proteases**  — `exam_bb90_9_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Differential methylation during gametogenesis; Prader-Willi syndrome (deletion of paternal 15q11-q13) vs Angelman syndrome (deletion of maternal 15q11-q13).
- [x] **635. Protein Sorting & Targeting: N-Terminal Signal Peptide, SRP & Sec61 Translocon**  — `exam_bb90_9_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Random transcriptional silencing of one X chromosome in female mammalian blastocysts; Xist long non-coding RNA coating inactive X, recruiting polycomb repressive complexes (Barr body).
- [x] **636. Nuclear Localization Signals (NLS), Importin Alpha/Beta & Ran GTPase Transport**  — `exam_bb90_9_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Distal enhancer-bound transcription factors looping long chromosomal distances to interact with the core promoter via the multi-protein Mediator complex.
- [x] **637. Mitochondrial Protein Import: N-Terminal Transit Peptide & TOM/TIM Machinery**  — `exam_bb90_9_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Denaturation (95 °C), Primer Annealing (55-65 °C based on Tm), Primer Extension (72 °C); 2^n exponential amplification; designing forward and reverse primers.
- [x] **638. Quantitative Real-Time PCR (RT-qPCR) & The 2^(-DeltaDeltaCt) Method**  — `exam_bb90_10_p1 / exam_bb60_10_p1`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* SYBR Green intercalating dye vs TaqMan fluorogenic probes; threshold cycle (Ct) inversely proportional to initial template copy number; quantifying relative gene expression.
- [x] **639. Recombinant DNA Technology: Restriction Endonucleases & Plasmids**  — `exam_bb90_10_p2 / exam_bb60_10_p2`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Type II restriction endonucleases recognizing palindromic recognition sequences; sticky ends vs blunt ends; plasmid cloning vectors (origin, multiple cloning site, selectable antibiotic marker).
- [x] **640. DNA Sanger Dideoxy Sequencing vs Next-Generation Sequencing (NGS)**  — `exam_bb90_10_p3 / exam_bb60_10_p3`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Dideoxynucleoside triphosphates (ddNTPs) lacking 3'-OH terminating chain synthesis; NGS sequencing-by-synthesis parallel flow cells yielding gigabases of data.
- [x] **641. Southern Blotting vs Northern Blotting vs Western Blotting**  — `exam_bb90_10_p4 / exam_bb60_10_p4`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Southern (gel electrophoresis and probe hybridization for DNA); Northern (evaluating RNA transcript size and abundance); Western (antibody detection of specific proteins).
- [x] **642. Enzyme-Linked Immunosorbent Assay (ELISA): Direct vs Indirect vs Sandwich**  — `exam_bb90_10_p5 / exam_bb60_10_p5`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Direct (labeled primary antibody); Indirect (unlabeled primary + labeled secondary antibody); Sandwich (capture antibody on plate, antigen, detection antibody; high sensitivity).
- [x] **643. Chromatin Immunoprecipitation (ChIP): Mapping In Vivo Transcription Factor Binding**  — `exam_bb90_10_p6`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Formaldehyde cross-linking protein to DNA in living cells, sonication to shear chromatin, immunoprecipitation with specific antibody, uncoupling, and PCR sequencing (ChIP-seq).
- [x] **644. Fluorescence In Situ Hybridization (FISH) in Clinical Cytogenetics**  — `exam_bb90_10_p6`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Fluorophore-labeled DNA probes hybridized directly to fixed metaphase chromosomes or interphase nuclei; detecting chromosomal microdeletions, translocations (BCR-ABL).
- [x] **645. Recombinant Protein Expression in E. coli: Inclusion Bodies & Chaperones**  — `exam_bb90_10_disc / exam_bb60_10_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* High-level expression driven by T7 RNA polymerase/IPTG induction; misfolded insoluble inclusion bodies requiring denaturing extraction and in vitro refolding.
- [x] **646. Gene Knockout Technologies: Homologous Recombination vs Cre-LoxP Conditional Knockouts**  — `exam_bb90_10_p7`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Conventional germline knockout vs Cre recombinase excising DNA flanked by loxP sites in a cell-type-specific or temporally controlled manner.
- [x] **647. Transgenic Organisms: Pronuclear Microinjection vs Viral Transduction**  — `exam_bb90_10_p7`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Microinjecting foreign recombinant DNA directly into the male pronucleus of a fertilized zygote; random genomic integration and mosaic expression.
- [x] **648. RNA Sequencing (RNA-seq) & The Cellular Transcriptome**  — `exam_bb90_10_disc / exam_bb60_10_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Total RNA extraction, ribosomal RNA depletion, reverse transcription to cDNA library, high-throughput sequencing, alignment to reference genome to quantify splice variants.
- [x] **649. Microarray Hybridization for Global Gene Expression Profiling**  — `exam_bb90_10_disc / exam_bb60_10_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Microscopic silicon chips spotted with thousands of single-stranded oligonucleotide probes; dual-color competitive cDNA hybridization (tumor vs normal).
- [x] **650. Flow Cytometry & Fluorescence-Activated Cell Sorting (FACS)**  — `exam_bb90_10_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Hydrodynamic focusing of cells in single file past laser beams; forward scatter (size), side scatter (granularity), multi-color fluorophore antibody sorting into viable pools.

### Cell Biology, Organelles, Membrane Transport & Microbiology (Topics 651 - 695)

- [ ] **651. Eukaryotic Plasma Membrane: Lipid Rafts & Bilayer Asymmetry**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Cholesterol buffering membrane fluidity, sphingolipids, GPI anchors, ATP-dependent flippases/floppases/scramblases maintaining asymmetric inner/outer leaflet composition.
- [ ] **652. Simple Diffusion, Osmosis & Aquaporin Water Channels**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Fick's first law of diffusion, osmotic pressure van 't Hoff equation (Pi = iMRT), aquaporin NPA motifs preventing proton conductance across cell membranes.
- [ ] **653. Facilitated Diffusion: GLUT Transporters & Ion Channels**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Carrier vs channel-mediated passive transport, GLUT1 basal vs GLUT4 insulin-dependent vesicle translocation, patch-clamp recordings of single-channel currents.
- [ ] **654. Primary Active Transport: Na+/K+-ATPase & Ca2+-ATPase (SERCA)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* P-type ATPases, E1/E2 conformational states, cardiotonic steroids (ouabain, digoxin) inhibiting Na+/K+ pump, thapsigargin SERCA blockade elevating cytosolic Ca2+.
- [ ] **655. Secondary Active Transport: Symporters (Cotransporters) & Antiporters**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Na+/glucose SGLT1 cotransport, Na+/Ca2+ NCX exchanger stoichiometry, electroneutral vs electrogenic coupled transport driven by electrochemical ion gradients.
- [ ] **656. Endocytosis: Clathrin-Coated Pits, Caveolae & Pinocytosis**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Receptor-mediated endocytosis of LDL, dynamin GTPase vesicle pinching, caveolin oligomers in lipid rafts, non-specific macropinocytosis fluid engulfment.
- [ ] **657. Exocytosis & Vesicle Fusion: SNARE Hypothesis & Synaptotagmin**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* v-SNARE synaptobrevin pairing with t-SNARE syntaxin/SNAP-25 four-helix bundle, Ca2+ sensor synaptotagmin, botulinum/tetanus neurotoxin endopeptidase cleavage.
- [ ] **658. Nuclear Envelope, Nuclear Pore Complexes & Nucleocytoplasmic Transport**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Nuclear pore FG nucleoporin repeats, importin-alpha/beta cargo binding, Ran-GTP/Ran-GDP asymmetric gradient driving directional nuclear import and export.
- [ ] **659. Rough Endoplasmic Reticulum: Signal Peptide Recognition & Translocation**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* N-terminal hydrophobic signal peptide, signal recognition particle (SRP) GTPase binding, Sec61 translocon channel docking, lumenal signal peptidase cleavage.
- [ ] **660. Protein N-Glycosylation & Quality Control in the ER Lumen**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Dolichol pyrophosphate oligosaccharide precursor transfer, calnexin/calreticulin chaperone folding, ER-associated degradation (ERAD) via retrotranslocation to ubiquitin-proteasome.
- [ ] **661. Smooth Endoplasmic Reticulum: Lipid Synthesis & Xenobiotic Detoxification**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Phospholipid/cholesterol synthesis, cytochrome P450 monooxygenase hydroxylation of lipophilic toxins, specialized sarcoplasmic reticulum intracellular Ca2+ sequestration.
- [ ] **662. Golgi Apparatus: Cis/Medial/Trans Cisternae & Vesicular Trafficking**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* COPI retrograde transport to ER, COPII anterograde transport from ER, O-linked glycosylation on Ser/Thr, mannose-6-phosphate tagging for lysosomal enzyme targeting.
- [ ] **663. Lysosomes, Acid Hydrolases & Sphingolipidoses**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Vacuolar V-ATPase proton pump maintaining acidic lumen (pH 4.5-5.0), glucocerebrosidase deficiency (Gaucher disease), hexosaminidase A deficiency (Tay-Sachs disease), LC3-mediated autophagy.
- [ ] **664. Peroxisomes: Very Long-Chain Fatty Acid (VLCFA) Oxidation & Catalase**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Peroxin biogenesis proteins (Zellweger spectrum), alpha-oxidation of branched-chain phytanic acid (Refsum disease), hydrogen peroxide (H2O2) generation and catalase decomposition.
- [ ] **665. Mitochondria: Double Membrane, Cristae & Mitochondrial Genetics**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Porin-permeable outer membrane, cardiolipin-rich impermeable inner membrane, maternal cytoplasmic inheritance of circular mtDNA, heteroplasmy threshold effect.
- [ ] **666. Cytoskeleton Microfilaments: Actin Dynamics, Treadmilling & Myosin**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* G-actin to F-actin ATP-dependent polymerization, critical concentration (Cc), barbed (+) vs pointed (-) ends, treadmilling equilibrium, cytochalasin vs phalloidin toxin mechanisms.
- [ ] **667. Cytoskeleton Intermediate Filaments: Keratin, Vimentin, Desmin & Lamins**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Non-polar rope-like tensile tetramers, desmosomal structural integrity in epithelial sheets, nuclear lamina phosphorylation and disassembly during mitotic prophase.
- [ ] **668. Cytoskeleton Microtubules: Tubulin Heterodimers & Dynamic Instability**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* GTP cap hydrolysis, catastrophe vs rescue transitions, kinesin anterograde (+) vs dynein retrograde (-) motor transport, paclitaxel microtubule stabilization vs colchicine depolymerization.
- [ ] **669. Centrosomes, Centrioles & Microtubule Organizing Centers (MTOCs)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* 9+0 triplet microtubule centriole architecture, gamma-tubulin ring complexes (gamma-TuRC) nucleating spindle microtubules, pericentriolar material duplication during S phase.
- [ ] **670. Cilia and Flagella Ultrastructure: The 9+2 Axoneme & Dynein Arms**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Radial spokes, nexin elastic links, axonemal dynein ATPase arms driving doublet microtubule sliding and ciliary bending, primary ciliary dyskinesia (Kartagener syndrome).
- [ ] **671. Cell Junctions: Tight Junctions (Zonula Occludens) & Epithelial Polarity**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Claudin and occludin transmembrane strands, blood-brain barrier and intestinal epithelial barrier sealing, apicobasal polarity maintenance, transepithelial electrical resistance.
- [ ] **672. Cell Junctions: Adherens Junctions & Desmosomes (Anchoring Junctions)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Cadherin homophilic Ca2+-dependent extracellular adhesion, catenin linkage to actin filaments; desmogleins/desmocollins linked to intermediate filaments, pemphigus vulgaris autoantibodies.
- [ ] **673. Cell Junctions: Gap Junctions & Intercellular Metabolic Coupling**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Connexin hexamers forming transmembrane connexon channels, passive intercellular diffusion of inorganic ions and second messengers (<1 kDa), cardiac intercalated disc synchronization.
- [ ] **674. Extracellular Matrix (ECM): Collagen, Elastin, Fibronectin & Integrins**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Triple-helix procollagen synthesis and vitamin C-dependent proline hydroxylation, tropoelastin cross-linking, fibronectin RGD motif, heterodimeric integrin focal adhesions.
- [ ] **675. Cell Cycle Phases: Interphase (G1, S, G2) & Cyclin-CDK Regulation**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Cyclin D-CDK4/6 phosphorylating retinoblastoma protein (pRb), E2F release, Cyclin E-CDK2 firing G1/S transition, Cyclin B-CDK1 (MPF) driving mitotic entry.
- [ ] **676. Cell Cycle Checkpoints: G1/S, G2/M & Spindle Assembly Checkpoint (SAC)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* ATM/ATR kinase DNA break detection, p53 stabilization, p21 CIP1/WAF1 CDK inhibition, Mad2 kinetochore tension sensing inhibiting APC/C until amphitelic attachment.
- [ ] **677. Mitosis: Prophase, Metaphase, Anaphase & Telophase Mechanics**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Chromosome condensation (condensin), kinetochore microtubule capture, APC/C-mediated securin degradation, separase cleaving cohesin rings, cytokinesis actomyosin contractile ring.
- [ ] **678. Apoptosis: Intrinsic (Mitochondrial) vs Extrinsic (Death Receptor) Pathways**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Bax/Bak pore formation, cytochrome c release, Apaf-1 apoptosome assembly, procaspase-9 cleavage vs FasL/TNF death receptor DISC activation of initiator caspase-8/10.
- [ ] **679. Cancer Biology: Oncogenes, Tumor Suppressor Genes & The Hallmarks of Cancer**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Constitutively active Ras GTPase, Myc amplification, Knudson's two-hit loss of Rb/TP53, telomerase TERT reactivation, VEGF angiogenic switch, epithelial-mesenchymal transition (EMT).
- [ ] **680. Stem Cells: Totipotency, Pluripotency, Multipotency & Differentiation**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Morula blastomeres (totipotent), blastocyst inner cell mass (pluripotent, Oct4/Sox2/Nanog), adult hematopoietic stem cells (multipotent), asymmetric self-renewing division.
- [x] **681. Bacterial Cell Envelope: Gram-Positive vs Gram-Negative Architecture**  — `exam_bb90_10_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Thick peptidoglycan with lipoteichoic acids vs thin peptidoglycan with asymmetric outer membrane, LPS endotoxin lipid A, periplasmic space beta-lactamase retention.
- [x] **682. Bacterial Motility: Flagellar Motor & Chemotactic Signal Transduction**  — `exam_bb90_10_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Basal body rotor embedded in cell envelope driven by proton motive force (PMF), counterclockwise run vs clockwise tumble, CheA histidine kinase and CheY response regulator phosphorylation.
- [x] **683. Bacterial Growth Kinetics: Lag, Exponential, Stationary & Death Phases**  — `exam_bb90_10_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Binary fission generation time doubling equation (N = N0 * 2^n), nutrient limitation, secondary metabolite and endospore formation in stationary phase, viable but non-culturable state.
- [x] **684. Bacterial Genetic Recombination: Transformation, Transduction & Conjugation**  — `exam_bb90_10_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Natural competence exogenous DNA uptake, generalized vs specialized bacteriophage transduction, F-plasmid sex pilus and high-frequency recombination (Hfr) chromosomal transfer.
- [ ] **685. Bacterial Transposons & Antibiotic Resistance Dissemination**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Insertion sequences, composite transposons carrying aminoglycoside or beta-lactamase genes, integrons and horizontal gene transfer via multidrug resistance plasmids.
- [ ] **686. Viral Structure: Capsid Symmetry, Enveloped vs Non-Enveloped Viruses**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Icosahedral vs helical capsomer geometry, host-derived lipid envelope susceptibility to detergents/heat, non-enveloped viral environmental stability, viral attachment glycoproteins.
- [ ] **687. Viral Classification: Baltimore Classification Scheme (Groups I - VII)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* dsDNA (I), ssDNA (II), dsRNA (III), positive-sense ssRNA (IV, directly infectious mRNA), negative-sense ssRNA (V, packaging RNA-dependent RNA polymerase), ssRNA-RT (VI), dsDNA-RT (VII).
- [x] **688. Bacteriophage Life Cycles: Lytic vs Lysogenic Phages & Prophage Induction**  — `exam_bb90_10_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Immediate virulent viral burst vs temperate lambda prophage integration into host bacterial genome, CI repressor maintaining lysogeny vs Cro transcriptional switch upon UV damage.
- [x] **689. Retroviruses: Reverse Transcriptase, Integrase & The HIV Replication Cycle**  — `exam_bb90_10_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Dimeric (+)ssRNA reverse transcription to proviral dsDNA, lack of proofreading error rate, integrase nuclear targeting, Gag/Pol/Env polyprotein processing by viral protease.
- [ ] **690. Subviral Pathogens: Prions & Pathological Amyloid Conformational Cascades**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* PrP^C alpha-helical native monomer template-directed refolding into insoluble beta-sheet-rich PrP^Sc fibrils, resistance to autoclaving and proteases, spongiform encephalopathy.
- [ ] **691. Subviral Pathogens: Viroids & Plant Pathogenesis Mechanisms**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Small circular single-stranded non-coding RNA molecules lacking protein capsids, autonomous rolling-circle replication via host RNA polymerase II, catalytic hammerhead ribozyme self-cleavage.
- [ ] **692. Cellular Senescence, The Hayflick Limit & Telomere Shortening**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Replicative arrest after ~50 population doublings, uncapped telomere persistent DNA damage response, Senescence-Associated Secretory Phenotype (SASP) pro-inflammatory cytokines.
- [ ] **693. Autophagy & Organellar Turnover: Mitophagy & Proteostasis**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* PINK1 kinase accumulation on depolarized mitochondrial outer membrane, Parkin E3 ubiquitin ligase recruitment, p62/SQSTM1 adaptor binding to LC3-II autophagosomal membranes.
- [ ] **694. Microbial Culture Techniques: Selective, Differential & Enrichment Media**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* MacConkey agar (bile salts/crystal violet inhibiting Gram-positives; neutral red lactose fermentation indicator), blood agar (alpha/beta/gamma hemolysis patterns), mannitol salt agar.
- [ ] **695. Innate Host Cellular Defenses: Interferon-Stimulated Genes (ISGs)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Viral dsRNA sensing by TLR3/RIG-I, Type I IFN-alpha/beta autocrine/paracrine signaling via JAK-STAT, 2'-5'-oligoadenylate synthetase (OAS) activating RNase L, PKR translation shutdown.

### Organ Systems Physiology & Developmental Biology (Topics 696 - 740)

- [ ] **696. Central Nervous System: Cerebral Cortex, Basal Ganglia & Diencephalon**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Frontal, parietal, temporal, and occipital functional specializations; basal ganglia direct (movement-promoting) vs indirect (movement-inhibiting) pathways; thalamus and hypothalamus.
- [ ] **697. Peripheral Nervous System: Somatic vs Autonomic Sympathetic & Parasympathetic**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Two-neuron autonomic circuits: thoracolumbar sympathetic (short preganglionic ACh, long postganglionic NE) vs craniosacral parasympathetic (long preganglionic ACh, short postganglionic ACh).
- [ ] **698. Reflex Arcs: Monosynaptic Stretch vs Polysynaptic Flexor-Withdrawal Reflexes**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Muscle spindle Ia afferents directly synapsing onto alpha-motoneurons (patellar stretch), Golgi tendon organ Ib reciprocal inhibition, flexor withdrawal with crossed extensor reflex.
- [ ] **699. Synaptic Transmission: Voltage-Gated Ca2+ Channels & Postsynaptic Potentials**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Pre-synaptic N/P/Q-type voltage-gated Ca2+ influx triggering vesicular exocytosis, ionotropic ligand-gated receptors generating fast EPSPs/IPSPs vs slow GPCR metabotropic modulations.
- [ ] **700. Neurotransmitter Homeostasis: Synthesis, Reuptake Transporters & Catabolism**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Choline acetyltransferase and acetylcholinesterase; tyrosine hydroxylase catecholamine pathway, monoamine oxidase (MAO) and catechol-O-methyltransferase (COMT); glutamate-glutamine cycle.
- [ ] **701. Endocrine Signaling: Peptide, Steroid & Tyrosine-Derived Hormone Classes**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Hydrophilic peptide hormones (insulin, PTH) binding plasma membrane GPCRs/RTKs; hydrophobic steroid hormones (cortisol, estrogen) binding cytosolic/nuclear receptors; thyroid hormones vs catecholamines.
- [ ] **702. Hypothalamic-Anterior Pituitary Portal System & Neuroendocrine Axes**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Hypophyseal portal system transport of releasing hormones: GnRH -> LH/FSH, GHRH -> GH, TRH -> TSH, CRH -> ACTH, dopamine tonic inhibition of prolactin secretion.
- [ ] **703. Posterior Pituitary Neurohormones: Oxytocin & Arginine Vasopressin (ADH)**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Synthesized in magnocellular neurosecretory cells of supraoptic and paraventricular nuclei, transported along hypothalamic-hypophyseal tract; oxytocin milk ejection, ADH V2 aquaporin-2 water reabsorption.
- [ ] **704. Thyroid Hormone Synthesis, Transport & Basal Metabolic Rate Regulation**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Thyroid follicular iodide trapping via NIS symporter, thyroperoxidase (TPO) iodination of thyroglobulin, pinocytosis, peripheral conversion of T4 to active T3 by 5'-deiodinase, genomic thermogenesis.
- [ ] **705. Calcium & Phosphate Mineral Homeostasis: PTH, Calcitriol & Calcitonin**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Chief cell CaSR sensing low extracellular Ca2+, parathyroid hormone (PTH) stimulating osteoclast resorption and renal 1-alpha-hydroxylase producing 1,25-(OH)2D3; calcitonin opposing bone resorption.
- [ ] **706. Adrenal Physiology: Cortical Zonation (GFR) & Medullary Catecholamines**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Zona glomerulosa (aldosterone), zona fasciculata (cortisol), zona reticularis (DHEA androgens); adrenal medulla chromaffin cells secreting epinephrine and norepinephrine under sympathetic control.
- [ ] **707. Endocrine Pancreas: Islet Architecture & Reciprocal Glucose Regulation**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Beta-cells: GLUT2 glucose uptake, ATP generation, ATP-sensitive K+ channel closure, depolarizing Ca2+ influx triggering insulin exocytosis; alpha-cell glucagon; delta-cell somatostatin inhibition.
- [ ] **708. Cardiac Conduction System & Pacemaker Action Potential Electrophysiology**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* SA node spontaneous phase 4 diastolic depolarization mediated by hyperpolarization-activated cyclic nucleotide-gated (HCN) funny current (If), AV node conduction delay, Purkinje fast conduction.
- [ ] **709. Cardiac Hemodynamics: Pressure-Volume Loops & Frank-Starling Law**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* End-diastolic volume (EDV) increasing myocardial sarcomere stretch and cross-bridge sensitivity; stroke volume (SV = EDV - ESV), ejection fraction, inotropic contractility vs afterload shifts on PV loops.
- [ ] **710. Vascular Hemodynamics: Systemic Vascular Resistance & Arterial Compliance**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Poiseuille resistance equation (R proportional to 1/r^4), arterioles as primary site of peripheral resistance, windkessel effect of elastic aorta dampening systolic pressure swings.
- [ ] **711. Microcirculatory Fluid Dynamics: Starling Forces & Capillary Exchange**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Capillary hydrostatic pressure (Pc) vs interstitial hydrostatic pressure (Pi); capillary oncotic pressure (pic) vs interstitial oncotic pressure (pii); lymphatic drainage preventing peripheral interstitial edema.
- [ ] **712. Erythrocyte Physiology, Erythropoiesis & Carbon Dioxide Transport**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Renal peritubular interstitial oxygen sensing, erythropoietin (EPO) stimulation, carbaminohemoglobin formation, chloride shift (Hamburger phenomenon) mediated by Band 3 (AE1) anion exchanger.
- [ ] **713. Hemostasis & Blood Coagulation: Primary vs Secondary Hemostasis**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Endothelial vWF binding platelet GP Ib, thromboxane A2 and ADP activation; extrinsic tissue factor-FVIIa complex, intrinsic FXII/XI/IX cascade, common pathway FXa converting prothrombin to thrombin.
- [ ] **714. Lymphatic System Architecture: Fluid Balance, Lipid Transport & Immunity**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Blind-ended initial lymphatic capillaries with overlapping endothelial microvalves, thoracic duct returning chyle and lymph to left subclavian vein, lacteals transporting dietary chylomicrons.
- [ ] **715. Innate Immunity: Physical Barriers, Phagocytes, NK Cells & The Complement Cascade**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Skin stratum corneum, mucociliary escalator, neutrophil respiratory burst (NADPH oxidase, MPO), NK cell missing-self MHC-I detection, complement alternative/lectin/classical C3b opsonization and MAC pore formation.
- [ ] **716. Adaptive Immunity: B-Cell Development, Somatic Hypermutation & Affinity Maturation**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Bone marrow V(D)J rearrangement, antigen engagement in germinal centers, AID-dependent somatic hypermutation of hypervariable CDR loops, competitive selection for high-affinity BCRs and plasma cell differentiation.
- [ ] **717. Adaptive Immunity: T-Cell Subsets, TCR-MHC Interactions & Thymic Education**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Cortical positive selection for self-MHC restriction, medullary negative selection against high-affinity self-antigen (AIRE transcription factor), CD4+ helper T-cell cytokines vs CD8+ cytotoxic perforin/granzyme release.
- [ ] **718. Pulmonary Mechanics: Diaphragmatic Contraction & Transpulmonary Pressure**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Boyle's law: diaphragmatic contraction expands thoracic cavity, dropping intrapleural pressure negative (-7.5 cm H2O), transpulmonary pressure gradient (P_tp = P_alv - P_ip) inflating elastic lungs.
- [ ] **719. Spirometry: Lung Volumes, Fractional Capacities & Obstructive vs Restrictive Patterns**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Tidal volume (TV), inspiratory reserve volume (IRV), expiratory reserve volume (ERV), residual volume (RV); FEV1/FVC < 0.70 in obstructive lung disease (asthma/COPD) vs reduced FVC with normal FEV1/FVC in restrictive fibrosis.
- [ ] **720. Pulmonary Surfactant & Alveolar Surface Tension: Laplace's Law**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Dipalmitoylphosphatidylcholine (DPPC) reducing surface tension at air-liquid interface; Laplace's law (P = 2T/r) explaining stabilization of smaller alveoli against collapse (atelectasis), infant respiratory distress syndrome.
- [ ] **721. Ventilation-Perfusion (V/Q) Matching & Hypoxic Pulmonary Vasoconstriction**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Apex high V/Q (dead space effect) vs base low V/Q (shunt effect); localized alveolar hypoxia inducing pulmonary arteriolar vasoconstriction to redirect blood flow to well-ventilated regions.
- [ ] **722. Central & Peripheral Regulation of Respiration: Chemoreceptor Reflexes**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Medullary central chemoreceptors sensing CSF [H+] derived from arterial PCO2 crossing blood-brain barrier; carotid and aortic bodies peripheral chemoreceptors sensing severe hypoxemia (PaO2 < 60 mmHg) and acidosis.
- [ ] **723. Renal Functional Anatomy: Cortical vs Juxtamedullary Nephrons**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Superficial cortical nephrons with short loops vs juxtamedullary nephrons with long loops descending deep into hyperosmolar renal medulla, specialized for maximum urine concentration.
- [ ] **724. Glomerular Filtration: Hemodynamic Determinants of GFR & Autoregulation**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Net ultrafiltration pressure P_uf = (P_gc - P_bs) - pi_gc; afferent vs efferent arteriolar resistance modulation; myogenic response and tubuloglomerular feedback mediated by macula densa sensing luminal NaCl.
- [ ] **725. Proximal Convoluted Tubule: High-Capacity Solute & Water Reabsorption**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Apical SGLT2 and Na+/amino acid cotransporters, Na+/H+ exchanger (NHE3), brush border carbonic anhydrase IV and intracellular CA-II reclaiming 85% of filtered bicarbonate, iso-osmotic water reabsorption.
- [ ] **726. Loop of Henle & Countercurrent Multiplication System**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Thin descending limb highly permeable to water via AQP1, thin ascending limb passive NaCl permeability, thick ascending limb active Na+/K+/2Cl- cotransporter (NKCC2) establishing medullary osmotic gradient.
- [ ] **727. Distal Convoluted Tubule & Collecting Duct: Fine-Tuning Electrolyte & Water Homeostasis**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Thiazide-sensitive Na+/Cl- cotransporter (NCCT) in DCT; principal cells in collecting duct with aldosterone-stimulated apical ENaC channels and ROMK; intercalated cells regulating H+/HCO3- secretion.
- [ ] **728. Renin-Angiotensin-Aldosterone System (RAAS) & Systemic Osmoregulation**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Juxtaglomerular granular cells secreting renin in response to decreased renal perfusion, angiotensin II causing systemic vasoconstriction and aldosterone release, restoring effective circulating arterial volume.
- [ ] **729. Gastrointestinal Secretions: Gastric Acid, Pancreatic Zymogens & Bile Salts**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Parietal cell apical H+/K+-ATPase proton pump stimulated by histamine (H2), gastrin (CCK-B), and ACh (M3); pancreatic acinar trypsinogen activation by enterokinase; bile salt micellar emulsification of dietary lipids.
- [ ] **730. Gastrointestinal Motility: Enteric Nervous System & Hormonal Modulators**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Myenteric (Auerbach's) and submucosal (Meissner's) plexuses, interstitial cells of Cajal generating slow wave basal electrical rhythm, gastrin stimulating motility, CCK and secretin delaying gastric emptying.
- [ ] **731. Hepatic Metabolism: Xenobiotic Detoxification, Urea Cycle & Bilirubin Conjugation**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Phase I functionalization (CYP450 oxidation) and Phase II conjugation (glucuronidation, sulfation); hepatic urea cycle converting toxic ammonia to urea; UDP-glucuronosyltransferase conjugating bilirubin.
- [ ] **732. Skeletal Muscle Sarcomere Ultrastructure & The Sliding Filament Mechanism**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* A-band, I-band, H-zone, and Z-disc spatial relationships during contraction; troponin C binding Ca2+, shifting tropomyosin aside, allowing myosin cross-bridge power strokes driven by ATP hydrolysis.
- [ ] **733. Excitation-Contraction Coupling: Transverse Tubules & Ryanodine Receptors**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Sarcolemmal action potential propagating into T-tubules, conformational change of voltage-sensitive dihydropyridine receptor (DHPR) mechanically opening sarcoplasmic reticulum ryanodine receptor (RyR1) Ca2+ release channels.
- [ ] **734. Smooth & Cardiac Muscle Physiology: Comparative Contractile Mechanisms**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Smooth muscle lacking troponin: Ca2+-calmodulin activating myosin light chain kinase (MLCK) to phosphorylate regulatory light chains; cardiac intercalated discs with gap junctions and desmosomes supporting functional syncytium.
- [ ] **735. Bone Remodeling: Osteoblasts, Osteoclasts & The RANKL/OPG Axis**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Osteoblasts depositing osteoid collagen matrix and secreting RANKL; RANKL binding osteoclast precursor RANK receptors promoting osteoclastogenesis; osteoprotegerin (OPG) acting as decoy receptor inhibiting bone resorption.
- [ ] **736. Integumentary System: Epidermal Stratification & Thermoregulatory Effector Mechanisms**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Stratum basale, spinosum, granulosum, lucidum, corneum; melanocytes shielding keratinocyte DNA from UV radiation; sympathetic adrenergic cutaneous vasoconstriction vs cholinergic sweat gland evaporative heat loss.
- [ ] **737. Male Reproductive Physiology: Spermatogenesis & Hormonal Feedback**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Seminiferous tubules: Sertoli cells nurturing developing spermatozoa and forming blood-testis barrier (FSH responsive, secreting inhibin); Leydig cells synthesizing testosterone (LH responsive).
- [ ] **738. Female Ovarian & Uterine Cycles: Folliculogenesis, Ovulation & Luteal Phases**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Follicular phase estradiol positive feedback triggering mid-cycle LH surge, ovulatory release of secondary oocyte, corpus luteum secreting progesterone to support secretory endometrium, luteolysis prompting menses.
- [ ] **739. Fertilization, Cortical Reaction & Blastocyst Implantation**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Sperm acrosome reaction penetrating zona pellucida, membrane fusion triggering intracellular Ca2+ wave and cortical granule exocytosis preventing polyspermy, blastocyst trophoblast invading endometrium and secreting hCG.
- [ ] **740. Embryogenesis: Gastrulation, Germ Layer Fate & Neurulation**  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Primitive streak formation, invagination producing ectoderm, mesoderm, and endoderm; notochord inductive signaling driving overlying neuroectoderm folding into neural tube; neural crest cell multipotency and migration.

## Section IV: Psychological, Social & Biological Foundations of Behavior (P/S)
### 260 Topics Across Psychology, Sociology, Biological Bases & Social Stratification

### Sensory Processing, Perception & Psychophysics (Topics 741 - 785)

- [x] **741. Bystander Effect, Diffusion of Responsibility & Social Loafing** — `ps_social / exam_ps60_p1`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Group size correlation with emergency intervention latency; evaluation apprehension, social loafing in collective physical vs cognitive tasks.
- [x] **742. Classical & Operant Conditioning Schedules, Extinction & Spontaneous Recovery** — `ps_learn / exam_ps60_p2`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Unconditioned vs conditioned stimuli/responses; acquisition, extinction, spontaneous recovery, stimulus generalization vs discrimination.
- [x] **743. DSM-5 Mood vs Anxiety Disorders & The Monoamine Hypothesis** — `ps_disorders / exam_ps60_p3`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Major depressive disorder vs generalized anxiety disorder criteria; serotonin/norepinephrine/dopamine depletion, SSRI receptor down-regulation.
- [x] **744. Signal Detection Theory (d', Criterion c) & ROC Curves** — `exam_ps90_p1`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Hits, misses, false alarms, correct rejections; sensitivity index d' vs decision criterion beta/c (conservative vs liberal); receiver operating characteristic curves.
- [x] **745. Stereotype Threat, Cognitive Load & Working Memory Capacity** — `exam_ps90_p2 / exam_ps60_p4`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Negative group stereotype priming impairing performance; central executive phonological loop/visuospatial sketchpad interference.
- [x] **746. Fundamental Cause Theory, Residential Segregation & Weathering Hypothesis** — `exam_ps90_p3 / exam_ps60_p5`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Socioeconomic status as distal determinant of health; flexible resources; residential redlining; allostatic biological aging.
- [x] **747. Elaboration Likelihood Model & Cognitive Dissonance Theory** — `exam_ps90_p4`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Central route (high motivation/ability, argument quality) vs peripheral route (heuristics, source attractiveness); dissonance reduction strategies.
- [x] **748. Operant Reinforcement Schedules & Mesolimbic Incentive-Sensitization** — `exam_ps90_p5`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Fixed-ratio, variable-ratio (highest rate and resistance to extinction), fixed-interval, variable-interval; ventral tegmental area (VTA) to nucleus accumbens dopamine.
- [x] **749. Dopamine Hypothesis of Schizophrenia: Mesolimbic vs Mesocortical Pathways** — `exam_ps90_p6`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Mesolimbic hyperdopaminergia causing positive symptoms (delusions, hallucinations) vs mesocortical hypodopaminergia causing negative/cognitive symptoms (avolition, flat affect).
- [x] **750. Dramaturgical Perspective & Impression Management (Goffman)** — `exam_ps90_p7`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Front stage performative behavior and impression management tactics (ingratiation, self-promotion) vs back stage private authentic behavior.
- [x] **751. Weber's Law & Difference Threshold (Just Noticeable Difference)** — `exam_ps90_discretes`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Delta I / I = k; constant proportional relationship between stimulus intensity and detectable change; limitations at sensory extremes.
- [x] **752. Theories of Emotion: James-Lange, Cannon-Bard & Schachter-Singer** — `exam_ps90_discretes`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Physiological arousal preceding emotion (James-Lange), simultaneous independent physiological and cognitive experience (Cannon-Bard), two-factor physiological arousal + cognitive label (Schachter-Singer), appraisal (Lazarus).
- [x] **753. Gestalt Principles of Visual Perception & Organization** — `exam_ps90_discretes`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Law of Prägnanz; proximity, similarity, continuity, closure, common fate, figure-ground segregation; top-down perceptual grouping.
- [x] **754. Sensory Adaptation vs Cognitive Habituation** — `exam_ps90_discretes`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Peripheral sensory receptor response reduction to constant physical stimulus (e.g., photoreceptor desensitization) vs central nervous system decreased behavioral response to repeated stimulus (habituation/dishabituation).
- [x] **755. Erikson's Psychosocial Developmental Stages across the Lifespan** — `exam_ps90_discretes`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Trust vs Mistrust, Autonomy vs Shame/Doubt, Initiative vs Guilt, Industry vs Inferiority, Identity vs Role Confusion, Intimacy vs Isolation, Generativity vs Stagnation, Integrity vs Despair.
- [x] **756. Kohlberg's Stages of Moral Development & Reasoning** — `exam_ps90_discretes`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Pre-conventional (obedience/punishment, self-interest), Conventional (conformity/good boy, law and order), Post-conventional (social contract, universal ethical principles); Heinz dilemma.
- [x] **757. Attribution Theory: Fundamental Attribution Error & Actor-Observer Bias** — `exam_ps90_discretes`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Overestimating dispositional vs underestimating situational factors in others; attributing own negative behaviors to situational factors while blaming others' dispositions.
- [x] **758. George Herbert Mead's Social Self: The 'I' vs The 'Me'** — `exam_ps90_discretes`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* The spontaneous, autonomous, un-socialized individual agent ('I') interacting dialectically with the internalized social expectations and attitudes of the generalized other ('Me').
- [x] **759. Institutional vs Individual Discrimination in Healthcare Delivery** — `exam_ps90_discretes`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Systemic organizational policies, resource allocation formulas, and geographic hospital closures vs interpersonal physician implicit bias, stereotyping, and microaggressions.
- [x] **760. Visual Phototransduction Cascade in Rods & Rhodopsin Activation** — `exam_ps90_2_p1 / exam_ps60_2_p1`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* 11-cis to all-trans retinal photoisomerization, opsin activating transducin (Gt), phosphodiesterase (PDE6) hydrolyzing cGMP, closure of cGMP-gated Na+/Ca2+ channels, hyperpolarization, decreased glutamate release onto bipolar cells.
- [x] **761. Auditory Hair Cell Mechano-transduction & Tonotopic Frequency Mapping** — `exam_ps90_2_p2 / exam_ps60_2_p2`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Organ of Corti basilar membrane: base narrow/stiff (high frequency) vs apex wide/compliant (low frequency); stereocilia tip-link deflection opening mechanosensitive K+ channels from high-K+ endolymph.
- [x] **762. Sleep Architecture, Polysomnography & Circadian Rhythm Entrainment** — `exam_ps90_2_p3 / exam_ps60_2_p3`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* NREM stages N1 (theta), N2 (sleep spindles, K-complexes), N3 slow-wave (delta), REM (beta-like EEG, desynchronized, rapid eye movements, muscle atonia); SCN retinal input regulating pineal melatonin release.
- [x] **763. Theories of Language Acquisition: Nativist, Interactionist & Learning** — `exam_ps90_2_p4 / exam_ps60_2_p4`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Chomsky Language Acquisition Device (LAD) and universal grammar with critical period vs Skinner operant conditioning and reinforcement vs Vygotsky social interactionist scaffolding and Zone of Proximal Development.
- [x] **764. Pierre Bourdieu's Theory of Cultural, Social & Economic Capital** — `exam_ps90_2_p5 / exam_ps60_2_p5`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Economic capital (financial wealth), Social capital (networks and institutional connections), Cultural capital (embodied linguistic habitus, objectified cultural goods, institutionalized degrees), habitus shaping dispositions.
- [x] **765. Talcott Parsons' Sick Role & The Medicalization of Deviance** — `exam_ps90_2_p6`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Structural functionalist sick role: institutional rights (exempt from normal social obligations, not held responsible) and obligations (desire to get well, seek and comply with medical expert care); medicalization expanding clinical control.
- [x] **766. Social Conformity, Groupthink & Group Polarization Dynamics** — `exam_ps90_2_p7`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Solomon Asch line judgment conformity (normative vs informational social influence); Janis Groupthink (illusion of invulnerability, self-censorship, mindguards); group polarization shift toward extreme collective consensus.
- [x] **767. Memory Interference & Forgetting: Retroactive vs Proactive Interference** — `exam_ps90_2_disc / exam_ps60_2_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Retroactive interference (new learning impairs retrieval of previously consolidated memories) vs proactive interference (previously encoded memories impair learning and retrieval of novel information). Ebbinghaus forgetting curve.
- [x] **768. Dual-Process Cognitive Models: Heuristics & Decision-Making Biases** — `exam_ps90_2_disc / exam_ps60_2_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* System 1 fast heuristic automatic thinking vs System 2 slow deliberate analytic thinking; representativeness heuristic (base-rate neglect), availability heuristic (salience and vividness), anchoring and adjustment bias.
- [x] **769. Neurobiology of Chronic Stress: HPA Axis Dysregulation & Allostatic Load** — `exam_ps90_2_disc / exam_ps60_2_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Sustained corticotropin-releasing hormone (CRH) and ACTH release driving hypercortisolemia; glucocorticoid receptor down-regulation; hippocampal CA3 dendritic atrophy, prefrontal cortex pruning, amygdala dendritic hypertrophy.
- [x] **770. Neurology of Emotion: The Limbic System & Klüver-Bucy Syndrome** — `exam_ps90_2_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Amygdala basolateral and central nuclei processing fear conditioning and emotional valence; bilateral anterior temporal/amygdala ablation causing Klüver-Bucy syndrome (hyperorality, psychic blindness, hypersexuality, loss of fear).
- [x] **771. Hemispheric Cerebral Lateralization & Split-Brain Syndrome** — `exam_ps90_2_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Corpus callosotomy severing interhemispheric communication; left hemisphere verbal specialization (Broca's and Wernicke's areas), right hemisphere visuospatial processing and face recognition; chimeric face experiments.
- [x] **772. Major Sociological Perspectives: Functionalism, Conflict & Interactionism** — `exam_ps90_2_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Macro-level Structural Functionalism (manifest vs latent functions, dysfunctions, equilibrium; Durkheim), Macro-level Conflict Theory (class conflict, resource competition, power differentials; Marx), Micro-level Symbolic Interactionism (shared symbols, everyday meaning-making; Blumer, Mead).
- [x] **773. Social Constructionism: Institutional Realities vs Brute Physical Facts** — `exam_ps90_2_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Ontological distinction between brute facts (independent of human cognition, e.g., gravitational physics) and institutional facts (created and maintained purely by human collective agreement and symbolic convention, e.g., currency, marriage, gender roles).
- [x] **774. Visual Anatomy & Optics of the Human Eye** — `exam_ps90_3_p1 / exam_ps60_3_p1`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Cornea refractive power, lens accommodation by ciliary muscles, iris pupil aperture control, aqueous humor flow through canal of Schlemm (glaucoma), vitreous humor.
- [x] **775. Retinal Circuitry: Photoreceptors to Retinal Ganglion Cells** — `exam_ps90_3_p2 / exam_ps60_3_p2`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Rods (scotopic vision, rhodopsin) vs cones (photopic vision, photopsins), horizontal and amacrine cells mediating lateral inhibition, on-center and off-center bipolar/ganglion cell receptive fields.
- [x] **776. Parallel Visual Processing: Parvocellular vs Magnocellular Pathways** — `exam_ps90_3_p3 / exam_ps60_3_p3`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Parvocellular (P-pathway: high spatial resolution, color, fine shape, slow temporal response) vs Magnocellular (M-pathway: high temporal resolution, motion detection, low spatial resolution).
- [x] **777. Visual Cortex Architecture: Feature Detection & Receptive Fields** — `exam_ps90_3_p4 / exam_ps60_3_p4`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Hubel and Wiesel discovery of simple cells (bar/edge orientation), complex cells (motion direction), and hypercomplex cells (length/angle stopping) in primary visual cortex (V1 / striate cortex).
- [x] **778. Theories of Color Vision: Trichromatic vs Opponent-Process Theory** — `exam_ps90_3_p5 / exam_ps60_3_p5`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Young-Helmholtz trichromatic theory (S, M, L cones sensing short, medium, long wavelengths) vs Hering opponent-process theory (red-green, blue-yellow, black-white opposing channels in ganglion cells and LGN).
- [x] **779. Depth Perception Cues: Monocular vs Binocular Mechanisms** — `exam_ps90_3_p6`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Monocular cues: relative size, interposition, linear perspective, aerial perspective, motion parallax, texture gradient; Binocular cues: retinal disparity (stereopsis) and binocular convergence.
- [x] **780. Visual Perceptual Constancies: Size, Shape & Color Constancy** — `exam_ps90_3_p7`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Cognitive mechanisms maintaining perception of object intrinsic properties despite drastic variations in retinal image size, viewing angle perspective, or illumination spectral wavelength.
- [x] **781. Auditory Peripheral Anatomy: Middle Ear Impedance Matching** — `exam_ps90_3_disc / exam_ps60_3_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Pinna and external auditory canal focusing acoustic pressure waves; tympanic membrane and ossicle lever system (malleus, incus, stapes) matching air-to-perilymph fluid acoustic impedance at the oval window.
- [x] **782. Central Auditory Pathway & Subcortical Sound Localization** — `exam_ps90_3_disc / exam_ps60_3_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Cochlear nerve projecting to cochlear nuclei, superior olivary complex computing interaural time differences (ITD) and interaural level differences (ILD) for azimuth localization, inferior colliculus, MGN to A1.
- [x] **783. Vestibular System: Semicircular Canals & Otolith Organs** — `exam_ps90_3_disc / exam_ps60_3_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Three orthogonal semicircular canals sensing rotational angular acceleration via ampullary cupulae; utricle and saccule sensing linear acceleration and gravity via otoconia-weighted gelatinous otolithic membrane.
- [x] **784. Somatosensory Mechanoreceptors: Tactile Transduction & Adaptation** — `exam_ps90_3_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Fast-adapting Meissner corpuscles (low-frequency flutter) and Pacinian corpuscles (vibration) vs slow-adapting Merkel discs (static pressure and form) and Ruffini endings (skin stretch and joint position).
- [x] **785. Pain Neurobiology: Nociception, Anterolateral System & Gate Control** — `exam_ps90_3_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* A-delta myelinated fibers (fast, acute, sharp pain) vs C unmyelinated fibers (slow, dull, burning pain); spinothalamic tract; Melzack and Wall gate control theory in spinal cord dorsal horn substantia gelatinosa.

### Cognition, Memory, Consciousness & Language (Topics 786 - 855)

- [x] **786. Proprioceptive Neuromuscular Sensors: Muscle Spindles & Golgi Organs** — `exam_ps90_3_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Intrafusal muscle spindles sensing muscle fiber length and rate of stretch (Ia and II afferents); Golgi tendon organs located at tendon junctions sensing active muscle contraction tension (Ib afferents).
- [x] **787. Olfactory Anatomy & Signal Transduction Mechanisms** — `exam_ps90_3_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Olfactory receptor neurons expressing G-protein coupled odorant receptors, Golf activation of adenylate cyclase, cAMP opening cyclic nucleotide-gated channels; axonal projection directly to olfactory bulb glomeruli bypassing thalamus.
- [x] **788. Gustatory System: Papillae, Taste Buds & Signal Transduction** — `exam_ps90_3_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Fungiform, foliate, and circumvallate papillae; ionotropic direct channel conduction (salty via ENaC, sour via H+ block of K+ channels) vs metabotropic GPCR cascades (sweet T1R2/T1R3, bitter T2R, umami T1R1/T1R3).
- [x] **789. Pheromonal Signaling & The Accessory Olfactory System** — `exam_ps90_3_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Vomeronasal organ (VNO) sensing chemical pheromones, axonal projections via accessory olfactory bulb to amygdala and hypothalamus regulating innate reproductive, territorial, and endocrine responses across taxa.
- [x] **790. Synesthesia & Intersensory Cross-Modal Integration** — `exam_ps90_3_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Involuntary sensory cross-activation where stimulation of one sensory or cognitive pathway leads to involuntary experiences in a secondary pathway (e.g., grapheme-color, chromesthesia); cortical cross-wiring.
- [x] **791. Psychophysical Thresholds: Absolute vs Difference Thresholds** — `exam_ps90_4_p1 / exam_ps60_4_p1`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Absolute sensory threshold (stimulus intensity detected 50% of the time) vs difference threshold (Just Noticeable Difference, JND); classical psychophysical measurement techniques: methods of limits, constant stimuli, and adjustment.
- [x] **792. Psychophysical Scaling Laws: Weber, Fechner & Stevens' Power Law** — `exam_ps90_4_p2 / exam_ps60_4_p2`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Fechner's logarithmic formulation (S = k * log(I)) assuming identical JND subjective magnitude; Stevens' power law (S = k * I^a) capturing subjective compressions (brightness, a < 1) and expansions (electric shock, a > 1).
- [x] **793. Visual Agnosias & The Ventral Stream: Prosopagnosia** — `exam_ps90_4_p3 / exam_ps60_4_p3`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Disruption of object recognition pathways in ventral stream; apperceptive agnosia (structural perceptual integration failure) vs associative agnosia (semantic disconnection); prosopagnosia from bilateral fusiform gyrus lesions.
- [x] **794. Auditory Agnosia, Pure Word Deafness & Amusia** — `exam_ps90_4_p4 / exam_ps60_4_p4`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Inability to recognize complex acoustic patterns despite normal pure-tone audiometry; pure word deafness (auditory verbal agnosia from superior temporal lesions) vs congenital/acquired amusia (musical pitch deficit).
- [x] **795. Top-Down vs Bottom-Up Perceptual Processing Mechanisms** — `exam_ps90_4_p5 / exam_ps60_4_p5`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Bottom-up data-driven processing assembling raw sensory sensory features into percepts vs top-down knowledge-driven processing utilizing expectations, conceptual schemas, and linguistic context to interpret ambiguous stimuli.
- [x] **796. Perceptual Set, Expectancy Effects & Context Effects** — `exam_ps90_4_p6`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Cognitive predisposition to perceive specific aspects of sensory data while ignoring others, modulated by emotional state, cultural background, motivations, and environmental situational priming.
- [x] **797. Developmental Depth Perception: The Visual Cliff Paradigm** — `exam_ps90_4_p7`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Eleanor Gibson and Richard Walk visual cliff apparatus demonstrating the emergence of depth perception, visual cues of cliff drop-offs, and social referencing in human infants and terrestrial animals.
- [x] **798. Motion Perception: Phi Phenomenon, Induced Motion & Motion Aftereffects** — `exam_ps90_4_disc / exam_ps60_4_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Perception of apparent continuous movement from rapidly alternating stationary light flashes (phi phenomenon); motion aftereffect (waterfall illusion) driven by neural fatigue of direction-selective cortical neurons.
- [x] **799. Selective Attention Theories: Broadbent, Treisman & Late Selection Models** — `exam_ps90_4_disc / exam_ps60_4_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Broadbent early filter model (all-or-nothing sensory bottleneck), Treisman attenuation model (unattended inputs attenuated rather than completely blocked, explaining threshold priming), Deutsch-Norman late selection model.
- [x] **800. Divided Attention & Cognitive Capacity Models: Kahneman's Resource Allocation** — `exam_ps90_4_disc / exam_ps60_4_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Multitasking performance decrements governed by task complexity, modal similarity, and total available cognitive resource capacity; controlled processing (effortful, conscious) transitioning to automatic processing with practice.
- [x] **801. The Cocktail Party Effect & Dichotic Listening Experiments** — `exam_ps90_4_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Selective auditory attention allowing focus on a single speaker amid noisy room; intrusion of salient personal stimuli (e.g., hearing one's own name) from the unattended ear in dichotic listening shadowing tasks.
- [x] **802. Visual Attentional Failures: Inattentional Blindness & Change Blindness** — `exam_ps90_4_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Inattentional blindness (failing to perceive an unexpected, fully visible object when attention is focused elsewhere) vs change blindness (failing to detect significant visual scene alterations across saccades or screen flickers).
- [x] **803. Visual Search & Treisman's Feature Integration Theory** — `exam_ps90_4_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Pre-attentive parallel search for distinct isolated single features resulting in instantaneous pop-out effect vs serial attentive search requiring focal spatial attention to bind conjunctions of multiple features.
- [x] **804. Hemispatial Neglect Syndrome & Spatial Inattention** — `exam_ps90_4_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Neurological condition following unilateral right inferior parietal or frontal lesions characterized by profound deficits in attending, responding, or orienting to sensory stimuli located in the contralateral left hemispace.
- [x] **805. Subliminal Perception & Implicit Cognitive Priming** — `exam_ps90_4_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Sensory registration of stimuli presented below the subjective threshold of conscious awareness; demonstration of behavioral and semantic priming effects in lexical decision and choice reaction time tasks.
- [x] **806. Multi-Store Memory Model: Atkinson-Shiffrin Architecture** — `exam_ps90_4_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Sequential memory processing pipeline: ultra-brief high-capacity sensory register, capacity-limited short-term store maintained by acoustic rehearsal, and indefinite consolidated long-term store.
- [x] **807. Sensory Memory Dynamics: Sperling's Partial Report Paradigm** — `exam_ps90_4_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* George Sperling's tachistoscopic matrix experiments showing visual iconic memory holds almost the entire visual array for ~250-500 ms before rapid decay; acoustic echoic memory persisting for 3 to 4 seconds.
- [x] **808. Working Memory Architecture: Baddeley and Hitch Multicomponent Model** — `exam_ps90_5_p1 / exam_ps60_5_p1`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Central executive supervisory attention system coordinating the phonological loop (inner voice/inner ear), the visuospatial sketchpad (mental rotation/spatial arrays), and the multidimensional episodic buffer.
- [x] **809. Working Memory Capacity Constraints: Miller's 7 +/- 2 & Modern Chunking** — `exam_ps90_5_p2 / exam_ps60_5_p2`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* George Miller's informational chunking hypothesis; Cowan's revision to 4 +/- 1 chunks under conditions preventing articulatory rehearsal; working memory capacity as primary correlate of general fluid intelligence.
- [x] **810. Explicit Memory Subtypes: Tulving's Episodic vs Semantic Distinction** — `exam_ps90_5_p3 / exam_ps60_5_p3`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Episodic memory (autonoetic conscious recollection of personally experienced events localized in subjective time and place) vs semantic memory (noetic general factual knowledge, vocabulary, and categorical concepts).
- [x] **811. Implicit Memory Subtypes: Procedural Memory & Repetition Priming** — `exam_ps90_5_p4 / exam_ps60_5_p4`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Non-declarative memory independent of hippocampus: motor skill acquisition (basal ganglia, motor cortex, cerebellum), repetition priming (neocortical perceptual tuning), and classical conditioning.
- [x] **812. Mechanisms of Memory Consolidation: Synaptic vs Systems Consolidation** — `exam_ps90_5_p5 / exam_ps60_5_p5`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Synaptic consolidation (rapid local LTP protein synthesis, dendritic spine expansion over hours) vs systems consolidation (gradual transfer of hippocampal-dependent traces to distributed neocortical networks over months/years).
- [x] **813. Long-Term Potentiation (LTP) & Cellular Neuroplasticity** — `exam_ps90_5_p6`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Coincidence detection by NMDA receptors requiring simultaneous postsynaptic depolarization (removing Mg2+ plug) and glutamate binding, Ca2+ influx activating CaMKII, trafficking AMPA receptors to postsynaptic membrane.
- [x] **814. Amnestic Syndromes: Anterograde vs Retrograde Memory Loss** — `exam_ps90_5_p7`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Bilateral medial temporal lobe lesions (Patient H.M.) causing dense anterograde amnesia (inability to form new declarative memories) alongside temporally graded retrograde amnesia sparing remote autobiographical memories (Ribot's law).
- [x] **815. Korsakoff's Syndrome: Thiamine Deficiency, Neuropathology & Confabulation** — `exam_ps90_5_disc / exam_ps60_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Chronic thiamine (vitamin B1) deficiency leading to hemorrhagic lesions of mammillary bodies and mediodorsal thalamic nuclei; severe anterograde declarative amnesia, lack of insight, and spontaneous confabulation.
- [x] **816. Alzheimer's Disease: Amyloid Plaques, Tau Tangles & Cholinergic Deficits** — `exam_ps90_5_disc / exam_ps60_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Neurodegenerative dementia characterized by extracellular beta-amyloid plaques (Abeta42), intracellular neurofibrillary tangles of hyperphosphorylated tau, degeneration of basal forebrain cholinergic neurons (nucleus basalis of Meynert).
- [x] **817. Semantic Networks & Spreading Activation Theory of Retrieval** — `exam_ps90_5_disc / exam_ps60_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Collins and Quillian hierarchical semantic model; Collins and Loftus network model where activation of a concept node spreads along associative links, lowering activation thresholds for connected semantically related nodes.
- [x] **818. Encoding Specificity Principle: Context- & State-Dependent Memory** — `exam_ps90_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Godden and Baddeley scuba diving context-dependent memory; retrieval success maximized when environmental context, internal physiological state (state dependency), or affective valence (mood congruency) matches encoding conditions.
- [x] **819. Serial Position Effects: Primacy, Recency & Asymptote Dynamics** — `exam_ps90_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* U-shaped serial position curve; primacy effect reflecting greater rehearsal and transfer into long-term memory; recency effect reflecting active retention in short-term working memory, abolished by delay tasks.
- [x] **820. Memory Distortions: Source Monitoring Errors & Cryptomnesia** — `exam_ps90_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Source monitoring framework (internal, external, and reality monitoring); source attribution failure resulting in remembering false facts or cryptomnesia (unconscious plagiarism where recalled memory is mistaken for novel creation).
- [x] **821. Reconstructive Memory & The Misinformation Effect (Loftus)** — `exam_ps90_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Memory as dynamic post-hoc reconstruction rather than exact playback; Elizabeth Loftus studies demonstrating post-event misleading questions distorting eyewitness testimony details (e.g., 'smashed' vs 'hit').
- [x] **822. Flashbulb Memories: High Subjective Vividness vs Objective Decay** — `exam_ps90_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Brown and Kulik flashbulb memory hypothesis for highly emotional, shocking historical events; empirical findings showing high subjective confidence and vividness persisting despite standard rates of forgetting and narrative distortion.
- [x] **823. Theories of Forgetting: Trace Decay Theory vs Interference Theory** — `exam_ps90_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Ebbinghaus exponential forgetting curve; passive degradation of physical memory traces over time vs active interference (proactive interference from prior memories; retroactive interference from subsequent learning).
- [x] **824. Prospective Memory: Event-Based vs Time-Based Prospective Retrieval** — `exam_ps90_5_disc`  
  *High-Yield Focus:* Remembering to execute future intentions; event-based prospective memory triggered by external environmental cues exhibiting higher reliability than self-initiated time-based prospective memory.
- [x] **825. Problem-Solving Approaches: Algorithms, Heuristics & Insight**   — `exam_ps90_6_p1 / exam_ps60_6_p1`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Exhaustive step-by-step algorithms guaranteeing correct solutions vs fast cognitive heuristics prone to systematic error; insight problem solving (Köhler's ape experiments) characterized by sudden restructuring of problem mental models.
- [x] **826. Obstacles to Problem Solving: Mental Sets & Functional Fixedness**   — `exam_ps90_6_p2 / exam_ps60_6_p2`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Duncker's candle problem demonstrating functional fixedness (inability to perceive alternative uses for an object beyond its traditional function); mental set (entrenched tendency to persist in applying previously successful strategies).
- [x] **827. Reasoning Biases: Confirmation Bias & Belief Perseverance**   — `exam_ps90_6_p3 / exam_ps60_6_p3`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Selective search, interpretation, and recall of information that corroborates pre-existing hypotheses while ignoring or discounting disconfirming evidence; belief perseverance maintaining convictions despite definitive empirical refutation.
- [x] **828. Judgmental Biases: Overconfidence, Hindsight Bias & Framing Effects**   — `exam_ps90_6_p4 / exam_ps60_6_p4`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Overconfidence phenomenon (subjective certainty exceeding objective accuracy); hindsight bias (the 'knew-it-all-along' illusion); Kahneman and Tversky prospect theory demonstrating framing shifts risk preference (risk-averse in gains, risk-seeking in losses).
- [x] **829. Theories of Intelligence: Spearman's General Factor (g) & Specific Factors (s)**   — `exam_ps90_6_p5 / exam_ps60_6_p5`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Charles Spearman's two-factor psychometric theory using factor analysis to demonstrate that positive correlations across diverse cognitive tests reflect a single underlying general intelligence factor (g).
- [x] **830. Theories of Intelligence: Cattell-Horn-Carroll Fluid vs Crystallized Ability**   — `exam_ps90_6_p6`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Raymond Cattell's distinction: fluid intelligence (Gf: raw non-verbal abstract reasoning and novel problem-solving, declining with age) vs crystallized intelligence (Gc: accumulated cultural knowledge and verbal skill, stable/increasing across adulthood).
- [x] **831. Theories of Intelligence: Gardner's Theory of Multiple Intelligences**   — `exam_ps90_6_p7`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Howard Gardner's modular pluralistic framework positing 8 independent domains (linguistic, logical-mathematical, spatial, musical, bodily-kinesthetic, interpersonal, intrapersonal, naturalistic) based on neurodevelopmental and savant profiles.
- [x] **832. Theories of Intelligence: Sternberg's Triarchic Theory**   — `exam_ps90_6_disc / exam_ps60_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Robert Sternberg's triarchic model: Analytical intelligence (academic problem-solving, evaluation), Creative intelligence (adapting to novel situations, insightful innovation), Practical intelligence (contextual street smarts, tacit knowledge).
- [x] **833. Emotional Intelligence: Salovey, Mayer & Goleman Dimensions**   — `exam_ps90_6_disc / exam_ps60_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Four-branch ability model: perceiving emotions in self/others, using emotions to facilitate cognitive thought, understanding emotional language and transitions, and regulating emotions to promote personal and social growth.
- [x] **834. Psychometrics of Intelligence: Normal Distribution & The Stanford-Binet/WAIS**   — `exam_ps90_6_disc / exam_ps60_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Wechsler Adult Intelligence Scale (WAIS) standardized bell curve (mean = 100, standard deviation = 15); definitions of intellectual disability (IQ < 70 with adaptive impairment) and intellectual giftedness (IQ > 130).
- [x] **835. Neural Correlates of Intelligence: Parieto-Frontal Integration Theory (P-FIT)**   — `exam_ps90_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Neurobiological models linking cognitive efficiency and high IQ to structural integrity, axonal myelination, cortical volume, and functional connectivity across frontal and parietal brain regions.
- [x] **836. Heritability of Intelligence & The Secular Flynn Effect**   — `exam_ps90_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Behavioral genetics monozygotic vs dizygotic twin studies demonstrating 50-80% IQ heritability; James Flynn's observation of secular gains in standardized IQ test scores (~3 points per decade) across 20th-century industrialized societies.
- [x] **837. Consciousness & Arousal: The Ascending Reticular Activating System (ARAS)**   — `exam_ps90_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Brainstem reticular formation projecting monoaminergic and cholinergic pathways to thalamus and cerebral cortex; clinical continuum of altered states: alertness, lethargy, stupor, coma, and brain death criteria.
- [x] **838. Electroencephalography (EEG) Characteristics of Brainwave Frequencies**   — `exam_ps90_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* High-frequency low-amplitude desynchronized Beta waves (13-30 Hz, active wakefulness); Alpha waves (8-12 Hz, relaxed eyes-closed); Theta waves (4-7 Hz, N1 sleep and drowsiness); high-amplitude synchronized Delta waves (0.5-4 Hz, N3 deep sleep).
- [x] **839. Sleep Architecture: NREM Stages 1-3 vs REM Sleep Electrophysiology**   — `exam_ps90_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* NREM stage 1 (theta waves, hypnic jerks); NREM stage 2 (sleep spindles, K-complexes); NREM stage 3 slow-wave sleep (delta waves, growth hormone release); REM sleep (paradoxical EEG, rapid eye movements, autonomic variability, somatic skeletal muscle atonia).
- [x] **840. Sleep Disorders: Dyssomnias (Insomnia, Narcolepsy & Sleep Apneas)**   — `exam_ps90_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Insomnia (impaired sleep initiation/maintenance); Narcolepsy (sudden daytime sleep attacks, cataplexy, hypnagogic/hypnopompic hallucinations, hypocretin/orexin peptide loss in lateral hypothalamus); obstructive vs central sleep apnea.
- [x] **841. Sleep Disorders: Parasomnias (Somnambulism, Sleep Terrors & REM Behavior Disorder)**   — `exam_ps90_6_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Non-REM slow-wave parasomnias (sleepwalking, night terrors occurring during N3 with post-event amnesia) vs REM sleep behavior disorder (failure of motor atonia during REM allowing physical enactment of violent dreams; alpha-synucleinopathy).
- [x] **842. Altered States: Hypnosis, Dissociation & Neurobiology of Meditation**   — `exam_ps90_7_p1`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Hypnotic susceptibility; Hilgard's neodissociation theory ('hidden observer') vs social cognitive role theory; mindfulness meditation inducing increased prefrontal alpha/theta coherence and reduced resting-state default mode network (DMN) activity.
- [x] **843. Psychoactive Drugs: Central Nervous System Depressants (GABA Agonists)**   — `exam_ps90_7_p2`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Ethanol, barbiturates, and benzodiazepines acting as positive allosteric modulators at GABA-A ligand-gated chloride channels; sedative, anxiolytic, and motor ataxia effects; dangerous cross-tolerance and life-threatening withdrawal delirium tremens.
- [x] **844. Psychoactive Drugs: Central Nervous System Stimulants (Monoaminergic Reuptake)**   — `exam_ps90_7_p3`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Cocaine (triple reuptake inhibitor of DAT, NET, SERT); amphetamines (reversing vesicular VMAT2 and DAT transporters, flooding synaptic cleft with dopamine/NE); caffeine (adenosine A1/A2A receptor antagonist); nicotine (nAChR agonist).
- [x] **845. Psychoactive Drugs: Opiates, Opioids & The Endorphinergic System**   — `exam_ps90_7_p4`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Morphine, heroin, and synthetic opioids (fentanyl) activating mu-opioid GPCRs; analgesia, euphoria, pinpoint miosis, severe constipation, and lethal respiratory depression reversed by competitive antagonist naloxone.
- [x] **846. Psychoactive Drugs: Hallucinogens & Cannabinoids (5-HT2A & CB1)**   — `exam_ps90_7_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* LSD, psilocybin, and mescaline acting as agonists at cortical pyramidal 5-HT2A serotonin receptors disrupting sensory gating; Delta-9-tetrahydrocannabinol (THC) activating presynaptic CB1 receptors modulating retrograde neurotransmitter release.
- [x] **847. Neurobiology of Substance Addiction: Mesolimbic Dopaminergic Circuitry**   — `exam_ps90_7_p5`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Ventral tegmental area (VTA) projecting dopamine to the nucleus accumbens shell and prefrontal cortex; chronic drug abuse driving downregulation of post-synaptic D2 receptors, physical dependence, tolerance, and compulsive craving.
- [x] **848. Linguistic Components: Phonology, Morphology, Syntax, Semantics & Pragmatics**   — `exam_ps90_7_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Phonology (discrete acoustic phonemes); Morphology (morphemes as minimal semantic units: free vs bound roots and affixes); Syntax (combinatorial grammatical rules); Semantics (literal meaning); Pragmatics (contextual communicative intent).
- [x] **849. Neural Substrates of Speech: Broca's Area, Wernicke's Area & Arcuate Fasciculus**   — `exam_ps90_7_p6`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Left hemisphere perisylvian language network: Broca's area (inferior frontal gyrus, Brodmann 44/45, speech motor output) linked via the arcuate fasciculus white matter tract to Wernicke's area (superior temporal gyrus, Brodmann 22, phonological comprehension).
- [x] **850. Clinical Aphasias: Expressive, Receptive, Conduction & Global Aphasia**   — `exam_ps90_7_p7`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Broca's expressive aphasia (non-fluent, agrammatic, halting speech with preserved comprehension); Wernicke's receptive aphasia (fluent, paraphasic word salad with severely impaired comprehension); conduction aphasia (intact comprehension and fluency, impaired repetition).
- [x] **851. Linguistic Relativity: The Sapir-Whorf Hypothesis**   — `exam_ps90_7_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Strong linguistic determinism (language strictly bounds and determines the horizons of human thought and cognitive capacity) vs weak linguistic relativity (linguistic categories, color lexicons, and grammatical genders influence and bias cognitive processing).
- [x] **852. Developmental Stages of Child Language Acquisition**   — `exam_ps90_7_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Infant cooing (2-4 months), canonical babbling (6-9 months), holophrastic single-word utterances (12 months), telegraphic two-word combinations (18-24 months), syntactic overregularization errors (e.g., 'goed', 'foots'), and vocabulary explosion.
- [x] **853. Bilingualism & Cognitive Executive Function**   — `exam_ps90_7_disc`
  *High-Yield Focus:* Bilingual advantage in executive inhibitory control, task-switching, and attentional focus arising from continuous implicit suppression of competing language representations; cognitive reserve delaying clinical onset of Alzheimer's symptoms.
- [x] **854. Comparative Communication: Animal Sign Systems vs Syntactic Recursion**   — `exam_ps90_7_disc`
